•	GLOBLA CATEGORIES



Sawa. Tunaanza sasa rasmi na Category 01, na tutaiweka kama mfano wa kiwango ambacho categories zote zitafuata. Sitaki tuandike list ya majina tu; tunafunga logic ambayo baadaye developer AI ataigeuza kuwa canonical taxonomy.
CATEGORY 01 — CHAKULA & VINYWAJI

Food → Local/Traditional
bila kuwa duplicated product taxonomy.

Sasa Attributes za Category 01
Badala ya kila subcategory kuunda attributes zake bila control, tutakuwa na canonical attribute library:
Food Attribute Library
├── Brand
├── Product Type
├── Variety
├── Species
├── Origin
├── Region
├── Grade
├── Quality
├── Freshness
├── Ripeness
├── Condition
├── Processing
├── Flavor
├── Ingredients
├── Size
├── Weight
├── Volume
├── Quantity
├── Unit
├── Packaging
├── Package Size
├── Organic
├── Frozen
├── Dried
├── Ready-to-eat
├── Expiry
├── Season
└── Custom
Lakini haitamaanisha kila product itaonyesha attributes zote.
Mfano:
Coca-Cola
Brand        → Coca-Cola
Flavor       → Cola
Size         → 500ml
Packaging    → Bottle
Condition    → New
Nyanya
Variety      → Roma
Size         → Medium
Freshness    → Fresh
Origin       → Tanzania
Weight       → 1kg
Mchele
Variety      → Saro
Grade        → 1
Processing   → Polished
Origin       → Mbeya
Weight       → 25kg

"Ni attributes zipi zinaruhusiwa kuwa filters?"

📍 Near me
💰 Price
✓ In stock
🥬 Fresh
🌱 Organic
🏪 Seller

________________________________________
VARIANT ENGINE
Hapa tunaweka rule muhimu:
Product inaweza kuwa:
NO_VARIANTS
au:
HAS_VARIANTS
Mfano:
Coca-Cola 500ml
→ No variants
→ Product itself = inventory item
Lakini:
Cake
→ Has variants
→ Size × Flavor
au:
Juice
→ Flavor × Size
au:
Rice
→ Package size
Sio kila option inakuwa variant.
________________________________________

User ataona tu kinachomhusu.

WEKA BIDHAA

[ Parachichi ]

[ Kg ] [ Piece ] [ Crate ]

Bei:
[________]

[________]
Hii ndiyo experience tunayotaka.

Category 01 canonical relationship
Mwisho wake tunataka data ionekane kimantiki hivi:
CATEGORY
Chakula & Vinywaji
        │
        ├── SUBCATEGORY
        │      Mboga & Fresh Produce
        │
        └── PRODUCT TYPE
               Nyanya
                  │
                  ├── ATTRIBUTES
                  │      Variety
                  │      Size
                  │      Freshness
                  │      Origin
                  │
                  ├── OPTIONS
                  │      Roma
                  │      Medium
                  │      Fresh
                  │      Tanzania
                  │
                  ├── FILTERS
                  │      Price
                  │      Variety
                  │      Freshness
                  │      Location
                  │
                  ├── FEATURES
                  │      Organic
                  │      Locally grown
                  │
                  └── VARIANT RULES
                         Size
                         Packaging
                         Weight
Hii sasa ndiyo level ninayotaka tuutumie kama standard ya taxonomy nzima ya SokoHai.
Hatua inayofuata

Je kitu fulani ni category au kinapaswa kuwa attribute/context?
•	Je attribute ni filter, variant-defining, zote, au neither?
•	Je seller wa kawaida anaweza kuipitia bila kuchanganyikiwa?
•	Je POS inaweza kuitumia?
•	Je Discover inaweza kuitumia?
•	Je online + offline + hybrid vinaweza kuitumia?
Baada ya hapo tunaingia Category 02: Kilimo & Agricultural Inputs kwa standard hii hii.





Twende. Category 02 tuiweke kwa ukaguzi wa kina kwa sababu Kilimo kinaweza kuwa na duplication kubwa kati ya input, equipment, service, mazao, mifugo na biashara ya jumla.
CATEGORY 02 — KILIMO & INPUTS ZA KILIMO
Canonical ID: agriculture_inputs
Display: Kilimo & Inputs za Kilimo
Lengo lake ni kushughulikia vitu vinavyotumika kuzalisha/kusimamia kilimo, si mazao yaliyokwisha zalishwa. Kwa hiyo:
•	Mbegu → hapa
•	Mbolea → hapa
•	Dawa za mimea → hapa
•	Zana za kilimo → hapa
•	Irrigation equipment → hapa
•	Greenhouse equipment → hapa
•	Seedlings/nursery inputs → hapa
•	Animal feed → Mifugo, si hapa
•	Mahindi yaliyovunwa → Mazao & Biashara ya Kilimo
•	Ng'ombe → Mifugo
•	Tractor → inaweza kuwa hapa kama agricultural equipment, lakini pia inaweza kuhusishwa na Vi-Industrial/Mashine kupitia classification.
________________________________________

1kg × package
2kg × package
5kg × package

Hybrid au Drought tolerant ni attributes/features, si lazima ziwe variant.

Variant rule
Mashine nyingi hazipaswi kulazimishwa kuwa variants.
Mfano:
Kubota Model A
ni product yenye model specification.
Ikiwa kuna configurations zinazouzwa tofauti:
Model A — 35HP
Model A — 45HP
ndipo variant inaweza kutumika.

02.14 Organic & Sustainable Farming Inputs
Hii isiwe category inayorudia bidhaa.

Context = Organic/Sustainable

badala ya kuunda bidhaa hiyo mara mbili.

Mipaka ya Category 02
Ili tusijenge duplication:
Kitu	Category
Mbegu	Kilimo & Inputs
Mbolea	Kilimo & Inputs
Dawa za mimea	Kilimo & Inputs
Jembe	Kilimo & Inputs
Irrigation	Kilimo & Inputs
Tractor ya kilimo	Kilimo & Inputs + equipment classification
Mahindi yaliyovunwa	Mazao & Biashara ya Kilimo
Mchele uliovunwa	Mazao & Biashara ya Kilimo
Ng'ombe	Mifugo
Chakula cha kuku	Mifugo
Huduma ya kulima shamba	Huduma
Kukodisha tractor	Huduma/Usafir kulingana na transaction
Uuzaji wa tractor kama machine	Equipment/Vehicle classification
Hii distinction itasaidia sana baadaye kwenye Discover, kwa sababu mtu anayetafuta "mbegu za mahindi karibu nami" hatakutana na mahindi ya chakula.
________________________________________
Category 02 sasa ina msingi wa kutosha kuingia kwenye master taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata ni Category 03 — Mazao & Biashara ya Kilimo, ambayo tutaitenganisha kabisa na inputs: mazao shambani, mazao yaliyovunwa, grains, legumes, cash crops, horticulture, spices, processed agricultural commodities, bulk/wholesale na grading/quality — huku Wholesale/Local/Export ikiwa context badala ya kurudia bidhaa.



Ndiyo. Tumeingia Category 03 — Mazao & Biashara ya Kilimo. Nimeifanya kwa kuangalia pia mgawanyo unaotumika Tanzania: mazao ya chakula, cash crops, horticulture, oil crops, pulses/legumes na maeneo ya uzalishaji. Wizara ya Kilimo pia hutenganisha kwa vitendo mazao kama mahindi, mpunga, muhogo, viazi, maharage, horticulture, kahawa, chai, korosho, pamba, tumbaku, sukari, n.k. (Kilimo)
CATEGORY 03 — MAZAO & BIASHARA YA KILIMO
Canonical ID: agricultural_commodities
Maana ya category hii
Hapa tunaweka zao lenyewe baada ya kuzalishwa, pamoja na biashara ya zao hilo:
Shamba
 ↓
Zao
 ↓
Harvest / Produce
 ↓
Grade / Quality
 ↓
Processing state
 ↓
Packaging / Quantity
 ↓
Retail / Wholesale / Bulk / Export
Hivyo:
•	Mbegu → Category 02
•	Mbolea → Category 02
•	Nyanya iliyovunwa → Category 03
•	Mahindi ya gunia → Category 03
•	Mchele wa biashara → Category 03
•	Kahawa green beans → Category 03
•	Korosho → Category 03
•	Huduma ya kusafirisha mahindi → Usafir
•	Huduma ya kusaga mahindi → Huduma
•	Tractor → Category 02 / equipment classification

________________________________________
Ukaguzi wa Category 03
Kwa sasa tumefunika:
Nafaka → Mikunde → Mizizi → Oil crops → Horticulture → Cash crops → Spices → Indigenous crops → By-products → Bulk/Wholesale → Grading → Processing states.
Na tumetenganisha vizuri:
Product ≠ Grade ≠ Processing State ≠ Trade Mode ≠ Certification ≠ Variant.
Pia tumetumia terminology inayolingana na mazingira ya Tanzania; Wizara ya Kilimo inaainisha mazao ya chakula na biashara pamoja na horticulture, oil crops na cash crops katika taarifa zake za sekta. (Kilimo)
Category 03 inaweza sasa kuingia master taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata ni Category 04 — Mifugo, ambapo tutahitaji kuwa makini zaidi kwa sababu itagawanyika kati ya mnyama hai, mifugo ya biashara, breeding stock, livestock products, feeds, equipment na veterinary-related products/services bila kuchanganya na Afya au Kilimo Inputs.



Ndiyo. Tuweke Category 04 — Mifugo kwa kiwango kilekile cha kina. Nimeangalia pia upande wa taxonomy kwa kuzingatia kwamba Mifugo siyo tu “mnyama”; kuna mnyama hai, breeding, nyama, maziwa, mayai, ngozi, manure/by-products, feed, equipment na huduma. Search ya vyanzo rasmi haikurudisha matokeo yanayoweza kutumika hapa, kwa hiyo taxonomy hii ni architecture ya SokoHai, si madai ya takwimu rasmi za sekta.
CATEGORY 04 — MIFUGO
Canonical ID: livestock
Kanuni kuu
MIFUGO
│
├── Wanyama Hai
├── Breeding & Genetic Stock
├── Nyama & Meat Products
├── Maziwa & Dairy
├── Mayai
├── Ngozi, Hides & Skins
├── Asali & Bee Products
├── Manure & By-products
├── Livestock Equipment
└── Livestock Trade Context
Lakini Feed, veterinary medicine na baadhi ya equipment zitahitaji relation na categories nyingine badala ya ku-copy bidhaa.

VARIANT RULE YA CATEGORY 04
Hapa kuna kanuni muhimu sana:
Animal = si variant
Ng'ombe A
ID: TZ-001
na
Ng'ombe B
ID: TZ-002
ni individual inventory/listing entities, si variants.
Meat = inaweza kuwa variant
Beef
├── Ribs
├── Fillet
├── Minced
└── Steak
ikiwa kila cut ina stock/price tofauti.

├── 6
├── 12
├── 30
└── 360

├── 500ml
├── 1L
├── 5L
└── 20L

RELATION ZA CATEGORY 04
Hii ndiyo sehemu ambayo itafanya taxonomy yetu iwe na nguvu:
MIFUGO
   │
   ├── Animal
   │      ↓
   │   Breeding
   │
   ├── Animal
   │      ↓
   │   Meat
   │
   ├── Animal
   │      ↓
   │   Milk
   │
   ├── Poultry
   │      ↓
   │   Eggs
   │
   ├── Bees
   │      ↓
   │   Honey / Wax / Propolis
   │
   └── Livestock
          ↓
       Manure / By-products
Na relationships zinaweza kuwa:
Animal → Product
Animal → Breeding Record
Animal → Livestock Product
Animal → Farm
Animal → Seller
Animal → Location
Bila kuanzisha taxonomy engine nyingine.
Kwa hiyo Category 04 imefunga maeneo makuu:
Ng'ombe → Mbuzi → Kondoo → Nguruwe → Kuku → Poultry nyingine → Sungura → Nyuki → Maziwa → Mayai → Nyama → Ngozi → Manure → Feed → Equipment → Breeding → Livestock Trade → Animal Services.
Na mipaka yake iko wazi:
Animal ≠ Meat ≠ Milk ≠ Egg ≠ Feed ≠ Veterinary Service ≠ Equipment.
Hii ndiyo foundation nzuri ya kuingia Category 05 — Uvuvi & Baharini, ambapo tutahitaji kutenganisha samaki hai, samaki fresh, samaki frozen, dried fish, seafood, aquaculture, fishing equipment, boats/gear na fishing services bila kuingiza duplicate za Food & Beverages au Usafir.



Ndiyo. Sasa tunaingia Category 05 — Uvuvi & Baharini. Hapa nimefanya ukaguzi wa ziada kwa sababu hii category ina overlap kubwa sana na Chakula, Mifugo, Kilimo, na Usafir. Tanzania pia inatambua rasmi aquaculture ya maji baridi na baharini, ikijumuisha sato, kambale, trout, milkfish, mullet, prawns, crabs, oysters na seaweed; kwa hiyo taxonomy yetu inapaswa kutofautisha capture fisheries, aquaculture, na aquatic products. (Mifugouvuvi)

MUHIMU: USALAMA NA COMPLIANCE
Fishery products si bidhaa za kawaida tu. Tanzania ina taratibu za vibali na nyaraka kwa baadhi ya shughuli za uvuvi/aquaculture na bidhaa zinazohamishwa/export; nyaraka rasmi pia zinaonyesha fields kama species, net weight, storage/transport temperature na approved establishment kwa baadhi ya fishery/aquaculture exports. (Mifugouvuvi)
Kwa hiyo kwenye taxonomy tutakuwa na:
Regulated Product?
        ↓
Compliance Required?
        ↓
Permit / Certificate
        ↓
Verification Status
Lakini: hatutajaza permit kama field ya lazima kwa kila samaki wa kawaida wa sokoni. Itakuwa conditional, kulingana na product, trade mode na transaction context.
________________________________________
RELATION NA CATEGORIES ZINGINE
Hii ndiyo architecture muhimu:
UVUVI
   │
   ├── Samaki
   │      ↓
   │   CHAKULA
   │
   ├── Aquaculture
   │      ↓
   │   KILIMO INPUTS
   │
   ├── Fish Feed
   │      ↓
   │   MIFUGO / INPUTS
   │
   ├── Boat
   │      ↓
   │   USAFIR / EQUIPMENT
   │
   ├── Fish Processing
   │      ↓
   │   HUDUMA
   │
   └── Fish Transport
          ↓
       USAFIR
Kwa hiyo hatutaunda duplicate product records kwa sababu tu bidhaa inahusiana na categories mbili.
________________________________________
Category 05 — QA result
Sasa tuna distinction hizi:
Capture Fish ≠ Aquaculture ≠ Fingerlings ≠ Fish Feed ≠ Fishing Equipment ≠ Fish Processing Service ≠ Fish Transport.
Na ndani ya bidhaa:
Species ≠ Size ≠ Grade ≠ Freshness ≠ Processing State ≠ Trade Mode ≠ Certification ≠ Variant.
Hii inafanya Category 05 iwe compatible na mfumo wetu wa:
Category → Subcategory → Product Type → Attributes → Options → Filters → Features → Variant Rules → Selector.
Pia inatuandaa vizuri kwa Category 06 — Misitu, Mbao & Bidhaa Asilia, ambayo itahitaji kutenganisha mbao/wood, timber, poles, charcoal/firewood, non-timber forest products, herbs/resins, bamboo/rattan, raw materials na finished products, huku tukiepuka kuingiza furniture ambayo tayari iko kwenye Nyumba & Samani.




Twende. Category 06 — Misitu, Mbao & Bidhaa Asilia ndiyo category ambayo lazima tuijenge kwa uangalifu zaidi, kwa sababu “forest produce” ni pana sana: timber, bamboo, charcoal, firewood, poles, fruits, gums, resins, fibres, medicinal plants, honey n.k. Tanzania Trade Portal pia inataja timber, bamboo, baobab powder, canes, charcoal, firewood, fruits, galls, gums na honey kama forest produce. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Pia TFS inaonyesha value chain ya forestry ikihusisha sawmilling, engineered wood, bamboo processing na resin tapping; kwa hiyo tutatenganisha raw forest material, processed material, na finished product bila ku-copy bidhaa. (Tanzania Forestry Services)

06.21 Forest Trade & Compliance
Hii isiwe product category; ni trade/compliance layer.
Tanzania Trade Portal inaonyesha registration ya forest-product dealers, timber-yard registration, transit passes, grading na export certificates kwa baadhi ya forest products. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo taxonomy itakuwa na conditional fields:
Trade Mode
├── Retail
├── Wholesale
├── Bulk
├── Farm/Forest Gate
├── Processor
├── Timber Yard
├── Export
└── Import
Na pale inapohitajika:
Compliance
├── Permit Required?
├── Registration
├── Certificate
├── Grade Certificate
├── Origin
├── Transit Documentation
└── Verification Status
Hizi hazitaonekana kwa kila bidhaa.

CATEGORY 06 — QA
Kwa hiyo sasa tumetenganisha:
Tree ≠ Log ≠ Timber ≠ Pole ≠ Engineered Wood ≠ Furniture
Bamboo ≠ Rattan ≠ Fibre
Charcoal ≠ Firewood ≠ Briquette
Raw Forest Product ≠ Processed Product ≠ Finished Manufactured Product
Forest Product ≠ Forest Service
Wild Harvest ≠ Organic certification
Traditional use ≠ Medical claim
Na muhimu zaidi, tumethibitisha kwamba taxonomy inaweza kushughulikia bidhaa za local/traditional/rural Tanzania bila kuifanya ionekane kama marketplace ya industrial products pekee. TFS na nyaraka za forestry za Tanzania zinaonyesha pia umuhimu wa NTFPs kama honey, mushrooms, medicinal plants, gums, resins, fibres, fruits na nyinginezo. (Maliasili)
Category 06 sasa iko kwenye kiwango cha kuingizwa kwenye master taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata ni Category 07 — Nguo & Mavazi, na huko ndipo tutaanza kuonyesha nguvu ya mfumo wetu wa Attributes → Options → Variants, kwa sababu tutahitaji Size, Color, Material, Gender/Age Group, Fit, Style, Pattern, Fabric, Custom-made, Ready-made, Local/Traditional wear n.k. bila kutengeneza variants zisizo na maana.



Twende Category 07 — Nguo & Mavazi. Hapa ndipo tunaweza kuifanya taxonomy yetu iwe very strong, kwa sababu Tanzania ina ecosystem ya pamba → yarn → fabric → khanga/kitenge → garment, pamoja na tailoring, uniforms na traditional wear. Tanzania Trade Portal yenyewe ina “Textiles & textile products (Vitenge, nguo)” kama trade group, huku TIC ikitaja cotton, spinning, weaving, textile na garments kama sehemu za value chain. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Pia Khanga si tu kitambaa cha kawaida: UNESCO inaielezea kama kitambaa chenye border, central design na ujumbe wa Kiswahili, chenye matumizi ya kila siku, sherehe na utamaduni. Hivyo tutaihifadhi vizuri bila kuunda category za kurudia. (UNESCO Intangible Cultural Heritage)

CONTEXT DIMENSIONS
Hizi hazipaswi kuwa categories:
Condition
├── New
├── Used
└── Refurbished

Production
├── Handmade
├── Machine-made
└── Factory-made

Trade
├── Retail
├── Wholesale
├── Bulk
└── Custom Order

Origin
├── Local
├── Imported
└── Made in Tanzania

Availability
├── Ready Stock
├── Made-to-order
└── Pre-order

Use
├── Casual
├── Formal
├── Work
├── Sports
├── Traditional
└── Ceremonial
Hii ndiyo itafanya taxonomy yetu isiishie kuwa na:
Traditional Clothes
Handmade Clothes
Used Clothes
Wholesale Clothes
Imported Clothes
kama categories 5 tofauti.
________________________________________
CATEGORY 07 — BOUNDARY CHECK
Cotton
→ Category 03 source / textile material
→ Category 07 when sold as fabric/clothing

Fabric
→ Category 07

T-Shirt
→ Category 07

Sewing Machine
→ Category 33 / Machinery

Tailoring Service
→ Huduma

Wooden Wardrobe
→ Category 09 / Furniture

Leather Raw Hide
→ Category 06 / Forest? NO — Category 04 livestock by-product / leather chain

Leather Shoes
→ Category 07

Second-hand Dress
→ Category 07 + condition=USED

Traditional Dress
→ Category 07 + context=TRADITIONAL

Custom Suit
→ Category 07 + mode=CUSTOM_MADE
→ may link to Tailoring Service
Hii ya mwisho ni muhimu sana: product na service zinaweza kushirikiana katika transaction bila kuwa kitu kimoja.
________________________________________
QA ya Category 07
Sasa tuna:
Fabric ≠ Garment ≠ Accessory ≠ Tailoring Service ≠ Sewing Machine.
Na:
Size ≠ Variant
Color ≠ Variant
bali:
Size + Color + attributes zinazofafanua SKU = Variant
Vilevile:
Traditional ≠ Category
Handmade ≠ Category
Mitumba ≠ Category
Wholesale ≠ Category
Custom-made ≠ Category
Hii ndiyo structure itakayomruhusu SokoHai kumsaidia seller kuchagua, badala ya kumlazimisha ajaze form kubwa.
Kwa mfano, seller akichagua T-Shirt, mfumo unaweza kuonyesha Size + Color + Material + Fit. Akichagua Kitenge, uonyeshe Fabric + Pattern + Length + Width. Akichagua Custom Suit, ubadilishe flow kwenda measurements/customization.
Na kwa sababu Tanzania ina textile chain kutoka cotton hadi spinning/weaving/textiles/garments, tutakuwa tumeacha nafasi ya baadaye kuhusisha raw textile → fabric → garment bila duplicate products. (Tanzania Investment Centre)
Category 07 iko tayari kwa master taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata: Category 08 — Urembo & Personal Care, ambapo tutatenganisha skincare, haircare, cosmetics, fragrances, barber/salon supplies, personal hygiene, beauty tools, natural/local products na beauty services, huku tukizingatia tofauti kati ya product, cosmetic claim, variant (shade/size), na service.




Ndiyo. Category 08 — Urembo & Personal Care tuiweke kwa usahihi zaidi, kwa sababu hapa kuna mpaka muhimu kati ya cosmetic product, personal-care product, beauty tool, service, na bidhaa yenye medical/therapeutic claim.
Kwa Tanzania, TBS ndiyo yenye jukumu la udhibiti wa cosmetics tangu 1 Julai 2019; TBS pia ina miongozo ya usajili wa pre-packaged cosmetics na inahitaji taarifa za ingredients kwa majina ya INCI pamoja na labeling inayotakiwa. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics) TBS pia ina standards maalum kwa skin creams/lotions/gels na bathing soap. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo hapa compliance isiwe category, bali iwe layer inayowashwa kulingana na product type.

COMPLIANCE LAYER — MUHIMU SANA
Hii ndiyo tofauti kubwa ya Category 08 na categories nyingi tulizopita.
Kwa product inayotambulika kama cosmetic:
productType
      ↓
complianceProfile
      ↓
Applicable?
      ↓
Required Fields
Mfano:
Skin Cream
→ Cosmetic compliance = YES

Face towel
→ Cosmetic compliance = NO

Hair Dryer
→ Cosmetic compliance = NO
→ Electrical compliance = YES/conditional

Herbal Medicine
→ NOT ordinary cosmetic
→ Medicine/herbal regulatory pathway
TBS inasema cosmetics haziwezi kuuzwa ikiwa marking/labeling yake haikidhi requirements husika, na guideline yake inahitaji ingredient declarations kwa majina ya INCI. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo Product Form inaweza kuwa:
PRODUCT
↓
Taxonomy
↓
Attributes
↓
Variant
↓
Inventory
↓
COMPLIANCE PROFILE
Compliance isiingie ndani ya Variant engine.
________________________________________
CLAIM ENGINE
Hii ni muhimu sana kwa beauty.
Seller akiandika:
“Moisturizes skin”
hii ni product claim.
Akiandika:
“Inatibu eczema”
hiyo si ordinary beauty attribute; inaingia kwenye regulated/medical claim detection.
Kwa hiyo AI helper inaweza kusema:
Claim detected:
Potential medical/therapeutic claim

Please review applicable product classification/compliance.
Badala ya AI kuamua kwamba bidhaa ni dawa.
TMDA inaeleza kuwa tangu 2019 cosmetics ziko chini ya TBS, huku TMDA ikibaki na medicines, medical devices na diagnostics. (Tanzania Defense Ministry)

FINAL BOUNDARIES ZA CATEGORY 08
Face Cream
→ Category 08

Face Facial Service
→ Huduma

Hair Dryer
→ Category 08 / Beauty Equipment

Salon
→ Business Type

Hairdressing
→ Huduma

Shampoo
→ Category 08

Herbal Medicine
→ Medicine/regulated pathway, not automatically cosmetic

Natural Oil
→ Category depends on intended product/use

Coconut Cooking Oil
→ Chakula

Coconut Hair Oil
→ Urembo

Perfume
→ Urembo

Perfume Bottle
→ Beauty Packaging / packaging supply

Soap
→ Personal Care if bathing/personal use

Laundry Soap
→ Home/Cleaning category

Second-hand Hair Dryer
→ Category 08 + Condition=USED

Imported Cosmetics
→ Category 08 + Origin=IMPORTED + applicable compliance
Na hii inatupa principle muhimu sana:
Product classification itategemea intended use + product type, si jina pekee.
Kwa mfano “oil” pekee haitoshi kujua category.
________________________________________
Mfumo wetu sasa unaanza kuwa mmoja kweli
Category 06 ilikuwa:
Species → Material → Processing → Grade → Size
Category 07:
Garment/Fabric → Material → Size → Color → Fit → Variant
Category 08:
Product Type → Composition/Function → Shade/Size → Variant → Compliance
Lakini zote zinatumia engine moja:
CATEGORY
   ↓
SUBCATEGORY
   ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
   ↓
ATTRIBUTE RULES
   ↓
OPTIONS
   ↓
FILTER RULES
   ↓
FEATURES
   ↓
VARIANT RULES
   ↓
COMPLIANCE RULES (conditional)
   ↓
PRODUCT
   ↓
VARIANT
   ↓
SKU
   ↓
INVENTORY
Hii ndiyo structure tunayotaka kuendelea nayo kwa Category 09 bila kutengeneza engine nyingine.
Category 09 inayofuata: Nyumba & Samani — tutajenga kutoka household goods, furniture, mattresses, kitchenware, décor, lighting ya household, storage, beds, sofas, tables, wardrobes, appliances zinazohusiana na nyumba, pamoja na custom-made furniture, na tutatenganisha vizuri material → furniture type → dimensions → configuration → variant.


Sawa. Category 09 — Nyumba & Samani tuiweke kama category ya home living, lakini tusichanganye furniture, construction materials, appliances, décor na services. TBS ina standards zinazohusu furniture, mattresses na household textiles, na HS/PVoC classification ya Tanzania pia inatenganisha wooden furniture, bamboo/rattan furniture, bedding na mattresses. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 09 — NYUMBA & SAMANI
NYUMBA & SAMANI
│
├── Samani
├── Bedroom
├── Living Room
├── Dining Room
├── Office & Home Office Furniture
├── Kitchen Furniture & Storage
├── Bathroom Furniture
├── Outdoor & Garden Furniture
├── Mattresses & Bedding
├── Curtains & Window Coverings
├── Rugs, Carpets & Mats
├── Home Décor
├── Lighting & Decorative Lighting
├── Kitchenware & Tableware
├── Home Storage & Organization
├── Household Textiles
├── Cleaning & Household Supplies
├── Home Safety & Utility Accessories
├── Home Improvement Accessories
├── Handmade & Traditional Home Products
└── Furniture/Household Trade & Custom Orders
Kanuni ya kwanza
Category 09 = kitu kinachotumika kuishi/kupanga/kupamba nyumba au furniture.
Kwa hiyo:
•	Cement → Ujenzi & Hardware
•	Refrigerator → Electronics & Appliances
•	Solar system → Nishati & Umeme
•	Wooden sofa → Nyumba & Samani
•	Wooden door → Ujenzi & Hardware
•	Tailor-made curtain → Nyumba & Samani + Custom
•	Carpenter service → Huduma
•	Raw timber → Misitu, Mbao & Bidhaa Asilia

09.2 MATERIAL ENGINE
Hapa tunatumia relationship na Category 06 badala ya ku-copy material taxonomy.
Furniture
   ↓
Material
   ├── Wood
   │    └── Species → Teak/Pine/etc.
   ├── Metal
   ├── Plastic
   ├── Glass
   ├── Bamboo
   ├── Rattan
   ├── Upholstery
   ├── Leather
   └── Mixed

Sofa

material = Wood
materialSpecies = Teak

09.22 HOME SETS
Hapa kuna concept muhimu:
Set si variant automatically.
Mfano:
Bedroom Set
├── Bed
├── Wardrobe
├── 2 Bedside Tables
└── Dressing Table
Product inaweza kuwa:
Bundle Type = BEDROOM_SET
Na components zika-reference existing products.
Hii inazuia duplication.
Bedroom Set
   ↓
components[]
   ├── productId: bed
   ├── productId: wardrobe
   ├── productId: bedside
   └── productId: dresser

09.26 PRODUCT vs CONFIGURATION vs VARIANT
Hapa tunahitaji distinction mpya muhimu:
PRODUCT
= identity

CONFIGURATION
= customer-selected design/setup

VARIANT
= inventory/SKU identity
Mfano:
Sofa
PRODUCT
"Modern Sofa"

CONFIGURATION
3+2+1

VARIANT
Grey / 3+2+1
ikiwa ina stock tofauti.
Lakini custom sofa:
PRODUCT
Custom Sofa

CONFIGURATION
Seats = 7
Fabric = Blue
L-shape
Width = 3.8m

QUOTE
TSh X
Hiyo si variant mpaka iwe standardized SKU.

09.29 COMPLIANCE / QUALITY LAYER
TBS inaeleza kuwa standards zinaweza kuwa mandatory pale bidhaa inapohusu afya, usalama, mazingira au athari kubwa kwa uchumi; kwa hiyo hatupaswi kuweka “certified” kama field inayomaanisha automatic approval. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

09.31 CONTEXT DIMENSIONS
Tusiunde categories za:
Modern Furniture

09.32 BOUNDARY CHECK
Raw Timber
→ Category 06

Wooden Table
→ Category 09

Wooden Door
→ Category 10

Kitchen Cabinet
→ Category 09

Kitchen Installation
→ Huduma

Refrigerator
→ Electronics & Appliances

Electric Cooker
→ Electronics & Appliances

Cooking Pot
→ Category 09

Solar Home System
→ Nishati & Umeme

Mattress
→ Category 09

Hospital Bed
→ inaweza kwenda Afya / specialized equipment

Office Desk
→ Category 09

Office Computer
→ Computers & IT

Sofa Cleaning
→ Huduma

Furniture Repair
→ Huduma

Furniture Making
→ Huduma + optional custom product listing

Bamboo Chair
→ Category 09
material = bamboo
TBS's current classifications explicitly recognize wooden, bamboo and rattan furniture as furniture classes, which reinforces this separation between material source and finished furniture product. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
________________________________________
09.33 SPECIAL RELATION — MATERIAL SOURCE
Hii ndiyo sehemu inayounganisha Category 06 na 09:
FOREST
  ↓
TIMBER
  ↓
WOOD MATERIAL
  ↓
FURNITURE
Mfano:
Teak
↓
Sawn Timber
↓
Furniture Material
↓
Dining Table
Product ya dining table inaweza kuwa na:
materialSource:
  type = WOOD
  species = TEAK
lakini haitengenezi copy ya Teak product.
________________________________________
CATEGORY 09 — FINAL QA
Tumetenganisha:
Furniture ≠ Raw Timber
Furniture ≠ Construction Material
Furniture ≠ Appliance
Furniture ≠ Furniture-making Service
Custom-made ≠ Variant
Configuration ≠ Variant
Variant = SKU/inventory identity
Material ≠ Category
Style ≠ Category
Handmade ≠ Category
Used ≠ Category
Wholesale ≠ Category
Traditional ≠ Category
________________________________________
Hivyo architecture ya Category 09 inafunga vizuri:
CATEGORY
  ↓
HOME / FURNITURE
  ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
  ↓
MATERIAL
  ↓
ATTRIBUTES
  ↓
CONFIGURATION
  ↓
VARIANT RULE
  ↓
VARIANT / SKU
  ↓
INVENTORY
Na kwa custom:
PRODUCT
  ↓
CONFIGURATION
  ↓
MEASUREMENTS
  ↓
CUSTOM OPTIONS
  ↓
QUOTE
  ↓
ORDER
Hii ni muhimu sana kwa SokoHai kwa sababu user wa kawaida ataweza kuuza sofa ya kawaida kwa stock, wakati fundi mwingine atauza “tengeneza wardrobe kulingana na vipimo vya mteja” kupitia engine ileile bila kutengeneza mfumo mwingine.
Category 09 imekaa kwenye canonical taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata ni Category 10 — Ujenzi & Hardware, ambayo itahitaji separation kali kati ya materials, tools, hardware, plumbing, electrical/building components, roofing, paints, doors/windows, aggregates, machinery, pamoja na units kama kg, bag, piece, meter, m², m³, na bulk sales.





Sawa. Tunaendelea na Category 10 — Ujenzi & Hardware. Hii ni category kubwa sana, kwa hiyo tutaiunda kama construction supply ecosystem, si tu “cement na vifaa vya ujenzi”.
TBS ina technical committees zinazotenganisha uashi, cement/concrete/clay products, plumbing/sanitary, timber na vipengele vingine vya construction; pia viwango kadhaa vya construction products vimetangazwa kuwa compulsory. Kwa mfano, TBS ina standards za cement, reinforcement steel, PVC cables, concrete blocks, roofing sheets, pipes na treated timber. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Hivyo Category 10 inahitaji Compliance Profile ya conditional, kama tulivyofanya kwa cosmetics.
CATEGORY 10 — UJENZI & HARDWARE

UNIT ENGINE — MUHIMU SANA
Category 10 ndiyo inatulazimisha tuwe na central measurement/unit engine.
WEIGHT
├── mg
├── g
├── kg
└── ton

LENGTH
├── mm
├── cm
├── m
├── inch
└── ft

AREA
├── cm²
├── m²
└── ft²

VOLUME
├── ml
├── L
├── m³
└── ft³

COUNT
├── piece
├── pair
├── set
├── box
├── pack
├── bundle
└── roll

CONSTRUCTION
├── bag
├── truck
├── tipper
├── pallet
└── sheet
Muhimu:
Unit ≠ Variant.
Mfano:
Cement — 50 kg bag
50kg inaweza kuwa selling unit.
Lakini:
PVC Pipe 20mm × 6m
inaweza kuwa SKU/variant kwa sababu dimensions zinafafanua stock item.

COMPLIANCE ENGINE
Category 10 ndiyo category nyingine ambayo compliance profile lazima iwe canonical.
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
APPLICABLE STANDARD?
 ↓
Compliance Profile
 ├── Standard Number
 ├── Certification
 ├── Certificate Number
 ├── Manufacturer
 ├── Batch/Lot
 ├── Country of Origin
 └── Verification Status
Mfano wa products ambazo zinaweza ku-trigger profile maalum ni cement, reinforcement steel, PVC cables, certain roofing products, concrete blocks na treated timber. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
TBS pia ina type-testing service inayoweza kutumika kuthibitisha quality, safety, performance au compliance ya construction products kabla ya kutumika/kusambazwa. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo:
“TBS Certified” si attribute ya kawaida ya seller.
Badala yake:
certificationStatus
certificateNumber
verificationStatus
________________________________________
PRODUCT CLAIM ENGINE
Mfano seller:
“Hii cement ni strongest kuliko zote.”
System isiweke:
Feature = strongest
kama fact.
Badala yake:
Seller Claim
au strength class/documented specification ikiwa ipo.
Vilevile:
“Hili bomba linastahimili pressure X”
inapaswa kuwa technical specification/documentation, si AI-generated assumption.
________________________________________
RELATIONSHIPS
Category 10 inahitaji connections:
PRODUCT
│
├── MATERIAL SOURCE
├── COMPONENTS
├── COMPATIBILITY
├── ACCESSORIES
├── RELATED SERVICES
├── INSTALLATION SERVICE
├── COMPLIANCE
└── INVENTORY
Mfano:
Toilet
 ↓
Compatible
 ├── Flush System
 ├── Connector
 └── Installation Service
Mfano:
Roofing Sheet
 ↓
Accessories
 ├── Roofing Screws
 ├── Ridge Cap
 └── Gutter
Hii baadaye itasaidia SokoHai kufanya smart cross-selling, bila kutengeneza duplicate products.
________________________________________
CATEGORY 10 — BOUNDARY CHECK
Raw Timber
→ Category 06

Construction Timber
→ Category 10

Wooden Sofa
→ Category 09

Cement
→ Category 10

Concrete Mixer
→ Category 10
→ inaweza relate Category 33 Machinery

Generator
→ Category 33 / Nishati depending classification

Solar Panel
→ Category 35 / Nishati & Umeme

Electrical Cable for Building
→ Category 10

Phone Charger
→ Electronics

Water Pump
→ Category 10 if construction/water-system context
→ Category 02 if agricultural irrigation context
Hii ya mwisho ni muhimu: same physical product inaweza kuwa discoverable across contexts bila ku-copy product.
Mfano:
Water Pump
Product ID = P123

Context:
Agriculture
Construction
Home Water
Taxonomy inaweza kuwa na:
intendedUse[]
badala ya product tatu.

________________________________________
Jambo muhimu tulilofunga hapa
Category 10 haitakuwa “hardware dumping ground.”
Tumeweka tofauti kati ya:
•	Material — cement, sand, steel, timber
•	Component — pipe, fitting, cable, door
•	Finished building product — block, window, roofing sheet
•	Tool — hammer, drill
•	Equipment — mixer, compactor
•	Consumable — screws, sealant, grout
•	PPE — helmet, boots
•	Service — installation, plumbing, construction
•	Trade context — retail, wholesale, bulk, contractor supply
•	Compliance — conditional layer
Na kwa kuwa TBS inaendelea kusasisha viwango na hata ina viwango vipya vya construction vilivyogazetiwa mwaka 2025, compliance engine yetu lazima iwe data-driven na updateable, si hard-code ndani ya product form. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Category 10 iko tayari kwenye canonical taxonomy.
Kinachofuata ni Category 11 — Electronics & Appliances, ambapo tutatengeneza distinction muhimu sana kati ya device/product, model, specification, compatibility, capacity, voltage, power, condition, warranty, accessories, refurbished/used, na hasa jinsi ya kushughulikia phone/TV/fridge/solar/device variants bila kuingiza kila specification kama variant.






Sawa. Tunaendelea na Category 11 — Electronics & Appliances. Hapa tunahitaji kuwa makini zaidi kuliko categories zilizopita kwa sababu electronics zina Model, Specification, Compatibility, Capacity, Power, Voltage, Storage, Condition, Warranty n.k. — lakini si kila specification inapaswa kuwa Variant.
TBS ina viwango vya lazima vinavyohusu aina mbalimbali za household electrical appliances, battery chargers, kitchen machines, fans, water heaters, microwave ovens, pumps n.k.; catalogue yake ya sasa pia ina standards mpya za batteries na commercial refrigeration. Hivyo compliance hapa iwe dynamic, isiwe hard-coded kwenye form. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

11.33 AI PRODUCT-FILLING BOT
Hii ndiyo category ambayo itafanya idea yako ya bot iwe powerful.
User aandike:
“Samsung TV 55 inch 4K smart, 120hz, mpya, milioni 1.8.”
AI haijengi taxonomy yake.
Inafanya:
USER TEXT
 ↓
AI EXTRACTION
 ↓
CANONICAL TAXONOMY
 ↓
STRUCTURED PRODUCT DRAFT
 ↓
USER CONFIRMATION
 ↓
SAVE
AI inaweza kutoa:
Category = Electronics & Appliances
Subcategory = TVs & Home Entertainment
Type = Smart TV
Brand = Samsung
Screen Size = 55"
Resolution = 4K
Refresh Rate = 120Hz
Condition = New
Price = 1,800,000
Lakini:
Model = ?
ikiwa haijulikani, bot inauliza, haiibuni.

11.36 COMPLIANCE ENGINE
TBS inaeleza kwamba standards zinakuwa compulsory hasa pale bidhaa inahusu afya, usalama, mazingira au athari kubwa kiuchumi; pia viwango vinaendelea kusasishwa. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo:
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
Compliance Rules
 ↓
IF APPLICABLE
 ├── Standard
 ├── Certification
 ├── Certificate Number
 ├── Manufacturer
 ├── Import/Origin Information
 ├── Model
 └── Verification Status
Hatuwezi kusema:
“Kila electronic lazima iwe TBS certified”
kwa sababu applicability inategemea product/type/regulation.
Badala yake engine iangalie:
productType
+
jurisdiction
+
current compliance rules
________________________________________
11.37 ENERGY PROFILE
Kwa appliances:
Energy Rating
Annual Energy Consumption
Rated Power
Standby Power
Voltage
Frequency
Hii ni shared profile.
Kwa mfano:
Fridge
→ Energy Profile

TV
→ Energy Profile

Air Conditioner
→ Energy Profile
________________________________________
11.38 VARIANT RULES — FINAL
TV
Model = identity
Screen Size = specification
Color = possible variant
Phone
Model = identity
RAM × Storage × Color = possible variant
Fridge
Model = identity
Color = possible variant
Speaker
Model = identity
Color = possible variant
Battery
Model/Part Number = identity
Capacity/voltage = specification
Cable
Connector A × Connector B × Length
can be variant if SKU/price/stock differs.
Remote
Model/Part Number
+
Compatibility
usually product identity, not variant.
________________________________________
11.39 CATEGORY BOUNDARIES
TV
→ Category 11

Phone
→ Category 12

Laptop
→ Category 13

Printer
→ Category 11

POS machine
→ Category 11

Wi-Fi Router
→ Category 12 / Communications context
But router classification needs to be finalized with Category 12.
Solar Panel
→ Category 35: Nishati & Umeme

Solar Inverter
→ Category 35
unless it is specifically sold as an electronic component/accessory, where cross-context discovery can expose it.
Industrial PLC
→ Category 11 + relationship to Category 33

CCTV
→ Category 11
Security context = true

Alarm System
→ Category 11
Security context = true

11.41 FINAL CANONICAL FLOW
CATEGORY
Electronics & Appliances
        ↓
SUBCATEGORY
TVs & Home Entertainment
        ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
Smart TV
        ↓
BRAND
Samsung
        ↓
MODEL
[Model]
        ↓
SPECIFICATIONS
55"
4K
120Hz
        ↓
OPTIONS
Color
        ↓
COMPATIBILITY
Apps / Mount / Connectivity
        ↓
VARIANT RULE
Only if SKU/price/stock differs
        ↓
SKU
        ↓
SERIAL/IMEI
if applicable
        ↓
INVENTORY
        ↓
WARRANTY
        ↓
COMPLIANCE
        ↓
DISCOVER
Hii ndiyo distinction tuliyokuwa tunaitafuta:
Taxonomy haipaswi kuwa database ya kila specification ya kila electronic.
Tunakuwa na:
CANONICAL TAXONOMY
        +
ATTRIBUTE LIBRARY
        +
SPECIFICATION PROFILES
        +
COMPATIBILITY ENGINE
        +
MODEL/PART-NUMBER ENGINE
        +
VARIANT ENGINE
        +
COMPLIANCE ENGINE
        +
INVENTORY ENGINE
Kwa hiyo Category 11 inatumia engines zilezile za SokoHai, haijengi engines mpya kila subcategory.
Na TBS yenyewe inaonyesha kwamba electrical/electronic standards zinaendelea kusasishwa—kwa mfano catalogue ya sasa ina standards za 2025 za batteries, vacuum cleaners, commercial refrigeration, huku draft standards za 2026 zikiendelea kwenye electrotechnical area. Hii ndiyo sababu compliance rules zinapaswa kuwa configurable/updateable, si kuandikwa moja kwa moja kwenye JavaScript ya product form. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Category 11 imefungwa kwenye canonical taxonomy.
Hatua inayofuata: Category 12 — Simu & Mawasiliano, ambapo tutatenganisha vizuri mobile phones, tablets, network devices, routers, modems, SIM/network-related products, phone accessories, wearables, na tutaanza kuunganisha IMEI / eSIM / SIM / network bands / carrier compatibility / RAM / storage / color bila kurudia Category 11.





Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na Category 12 — Simu & Mawasiliano.
Hapa kuna correction muhimu kutoka Category 11: Category 12 isiwe “phone accessories” tu. Inapaswa kuwa communication ecosystem: simu, tablets, routers/modems, network devices, SIM/eSIM-related products, wearables za communication, antennas na communication accessories. TCRA kwa sasa ina Type Approval Guidelines za vifaa vya mawasiliano, na FAQ yake inaeleza kuwa kwa ujumla vifaa vya mawasiliano vya wired/wireless vinahitaji approval kabla ya kuingia soko la Tanzania; mifano yake inajumuisha simu, tablets, modems, laptops, GPS trackers, short-range communication devices na IoT devices. (TCRA)
Pia TCRA ina huduma ya IMEI verification inayolinganishia IMEI na model, manufacturer na frequency bands. (TCRA)

12.2 MODEL ≠ VARIANT
Hii ni muhimu sana.
Mfano:
Samsung Model X
ina:
RAM = 8GB
Storage = 256GB
Color = Black
Lakini:
Samsung Model X
8GB / 256GB / Black
8GB / 256GB / Blue
12GB / 512GB / Black
zinaweza kuwa variants ikiwa kila configuration ina SKU, stock au bei tofauti.
Kwa hiyo:
PRODUCT
 ↓
MODEL
 ↓
CONFIGURATION
 ↓
VARIANT
 ↓
SKU
 ↓
INVENTORY
________________________________________
12.3 IMEI ENGINE
Hapa hatutumii IMEI kama variant.
PRODUCT
   ↓
MODEL
   ↓
SKU
   ↓
PHYSICAL DEVICE
   ↓
IMEI
Mfano:
Model = ABC123
SKU = ABC123-8-256-BLK

Unit 1 → IMEI 123...
Unit 2 → IMEI 456...
Unit 3 → IMEI 789...
IMEI ni identity ya kifaa halisi.
TCRA inaeleza kwamba kila cellular mobile terminal inapaswa kuwa na unique IMEI, na huduma yake ya verification huonyesha model, manufacturer na frequency bands zinazohusiana na IMEI. (TCRA)
________________________________________

Brand
Network Compatibility
Condition
Restricted:
IMEI
Serial Number
Seller inventory
Warranty record
Transaction record
Kwa hiyo:
PUBLIC PRODUCT
        ↓
PRIVATE UNIT RECORD
        ↓
IMEI

12.5 FREQUENCY BAND ENGINE
Hii ni muhimu sana kwa Tanzania na devices za import.
Network
├── 2G
├── 3G
├── 4G
├── 5G
└── Other
Na:
Frequency Bands
├── GSM
├── UMTS
├── LTE
└── NR
Mfano:
Device
 ↓
Supported Bands
 ↓
Country/Network Compatibility
Usiweke:
“Works everywhere”
kama fact.
Badala yake:
Supported Bands
+
Network Compatibility

12.23 COMMUNICATION DEVICE COMPLIANCE
Hapa tuna layers mbili tofauti:
TBS
 ↓
Product / Electrical Safety Standards

TCRA
 ↓
Electronic Communication Type Approval
Si kitu kimoja.
TCRA inaeleza type approval kama mchakato wa kuhakikisha kifaa kinafikia minimum technical/regulatory requirements, na guidelines zake zinaweka market-surveillance na type-approval labelling requirements. (TCRA)
Kwa hiyo SokoHai:
COMPLIANCE PROFILE
├── TBS
│   ├── Standard
│   ├── Certification
│   └── Status
│
└── TCRA
    ├── Type Approval Applicable
    ├── Approval Number
    ├── Approval Status
    └── Label Verification
Usiweke TCRA Type Approval kama attribute ya kila electronic.
Rule engine ndiyo itaamua kama product type ina-trigger profile.
________________________________________
12.24 TYPE APPROVAL — PRODUCT LEVEL
TCRA FAQ inaeleza kuwa certificate haiwezi kutolewa kwa “aina nyingi” za kifaa kwa pamoja; approval hutolewa kwa msingi wa aina ya kifaa. Pia approval inatakiwa kabla kifaa husika hakijaingia Tanzania. (TCRA)
Kwa hiyo model record inaweza kuwa:
Communication Device
        ↓
Manufacturer
        ↓
Model
        ↓
Type Approval Record
na si:
Seller says:
"TCRA Approved = Yes"
pekee.
________________________________________
12.25 TYPE APPROVAL + MODEL CHANGES
TCRA guidelines zinaeleza kwamba mabadiliko fulani ya brand, product name, model number, function au mambo yanayoathiri compliance yanaweza kuhitaji application mpya. (TCRA)
Hii ina maana SokoHai isijenge:
Brand = Samsung
TCRA Approved = TRUE
kama permanent truth.
Badala yake:
TYPE_APPROVAL_RECORD
{
  manufacturer
  brand
  model
  approvalNumber
  status
  effectiveDate
  source
}
________________________________________

        ├── Routers
        ├── Modems
        ├── Network
        ├── GPS
        ├── IoT
        ├── Radios
        └── Communication Accessories
Na kwa Tanzania, TCRA compliance iwe separate regulatory layer, kwa sababu Type Approval ya communication equipment si sawa na spectrum licensing, na TCRA inaendelea kuchapisha technical specifications mpya—ikiwemo Cellular IoT devices mwaka 2026. (TCRA)
Category 12 imekamilika kwenye kiwango hiki.
Hatua inayofuata ni Category 13 — Computers & IT, ambayo itakuwa na mfumo wa kina zaidi wa Laptop/Desktop/Workstation/Server/Monitor/Printer/Storage/Networking/Components + CPU/RAM/GPU/Storage/Ports/OS/Compatibility + refurbished/used + serial/service tags, huku tukiepuka kurudia network engine tuliyoanzisha hapa.



Ndiyo. Sasa tunaingia Category 13 — Computers & IT. Nimeangalia pia upande wa Tanzania kwa sababu hapa kuna overlap na Category 12: TCRA kwa sasa ina records za Personal Computers kama equipment inayopata Type Approval, na guidelines zake zinajumuisha computers/laptops miongoni mwa electronic communications equipment inayoweza kuhitaji approval. TBS pia ina compulsory standards zinazohusu baadhi ya IT equipment, ikiwemo notebook power supplies, large data-storage equipment na safety ya information-technology equipment. (TCRA)
CATEGORY 13 — COMPUTERS & IT
Lengo si kutengeneza “computer category” ya kawaida tu. Tunataka iweze kushughulikia:
•	Laptop
•	Desktop
•	Workstation
•	Server
•	Mini PC
•	All-in-One
•	Thin Client
•	Computer components
•	Storage
•	RAM
•	CPU
•	GPU
•	Motherboard
•	Monitor
•	Printers & scanners
•	POS/computing devices
•	Networking/IT equipment
•	Accessories
•	Software/licenses kama digital products
•	Used/refurbished computers
•	Custom-built computers
Na bado itumie engines zilezile tulizojenga kwenye categories 11–12.

13.34 TYPE APPROVAL / COMPLIANCE
Kwa computer inayowasiliana na electronic communications networks, TCRA Type Approval inaweza kuwa relevant; TCRA currently lists Personal Computers as a Type Approval category, na current 2026 records zinaonyesha approvals za Personal Computers. (TCRA)
Kwa hiyo:
COMPLIANCE
├── TCRA
│   ├── Applicable
│   ├── Approval Number
│   └── Status
│
└── TBS
    ├── Applicable
    ├── Standard
    ├── Certification
    └── Status
Lakini conditional.
Si kila field ionekane kwa kila laptop.

→ Computers & IT

→ Laptops

→ HP

→ EliteBook [exact model missing]

→ Intel Core i5
→ 11th Gen

→ 16GB

→ 512GB SSD

→ Used

→ 78%

Boundary ya Category 13 sasa
Phone
→ 12

Router/Modem
→ 12

Laptop
→ 13

Desktop
→ 13

Server
→ 13

RAM
→ 13

CPU
→ 13

GPU
→ 13

SSD/HDD
→ 13

→ 13

→ 13

→ 13

→ 13

IT installation/repair
→ Huduma

Software subscription
→ Digital Products & Online Services

Industrial controller
→ 13 + possible relation to 33

Solar computer backup
→ shared with 35 where appropriate
Muhimu zaidi
Kwa sasa Categories 10 → 11 → 12 → 13 zinaanza kuonyesha architecture ileile:
CATEGORY
   ↓
SUBCATEGORY
   ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
   ↓
ATTRIBUTES
   ↓
OPTIONS
   ↓
FILTERS
   ↓
FEATURES
   ↓
CONFIGURATION
   ↓
VARIANT RULE
   ↓
SKU
   ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
   ↓
INVENTORY
   ↓
COMPLIANCE
   ↓
DISCOVER
Na Compatibility, Measurement, Color, Size, Condition, Warranty, Compliance na Unit zote ni shared engines — hatutengenezi engine mpya kila category.
TCRA pia inaonyesha kwa sasa categories za approval kama Personal Computers, Mobile Phones, Data Modems, Wireless Modules, Short Range Devices na Core Network Switching Equipment, jambo linalothibitisha kwamba compliance layer yetu inapaswa kuwa product-type driven, si category = electronics → TCRA=true. (TCRA)
Category 13 imekamilika. inayofuata ni Category 14 — Magari & Vyombo, ambapo tutahitaji kutenganisha kwa nguvu vehicle identity, make/model/year, body type, engine, fuel, transmission, mileage, condition, ownership/documentation, spare parts compatibility na vehicle-specific variants bila kuchanganya na Category 15 ya vipuri.






Sawa. Sasa Category 14 — MAGARI & VYOMBO tuiweke kwa kiwango cha mfumo mzima, kwa sababu hapa hatutaki kuifanya kama bidhaa ya kawaida. Vehicle ina identity ya kipekee, historia, documents, mileage, ownership, specifications na compliance.
TBS kwa sasa ina standard ya vehicle homologation kwa magari mapya ambayo hayajawahi kusajiliwa/licensed katika nchi yoyote, na pia ina standards zinazohusu motorcycles na registration plates. TBS pia inaorodhesha baadhi ya vehicles chini ya vehicle inspection scheme. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

14.46 COMPLIANCE ENGINE

└── Other Applicable Requirement

Inventory Authority → shared
Business/Store      → shared

Search/Discover      → shared
Hii ndiyo tofauti muhimu kati ya taxonomy nzuri na kuandika tu list ya categories: Category 14 sasa inaweza kushughulikia gari moja la mtu binafsi, stock ya dealer, used vehicle, motorcycle, truck, tractor, EV, trailer au boat, bila kuanzisha database engine mpya kwa kila aina.
Category 15 inayofuata ni VIPURI & ACCESSORIES — hapo tutajenga Part Number → Part Type → Vehicle Compatibility → OEM/Aftermarket → Position → Left/Right → Front/Rear → Model/Year/Engine → Variant → Stock, ambayo itakuwa moja ya engines muhimu sana kwa marketplace ya Tanzania.



Ndiyo. Sasa Category 15 — VIPURI & ACCESSORIES. Hii ndiyo category ambayo tunapaswa kuijenga kwa umakini zaidi kuliko kuandika tu “brake pads, filters, tyres…” kwa sababu spare part identity inategemea compatibility.
TBS inaonyesha kuwa parts za motor vehicles ziko kwenye mfumo wa conformity/PVoC, na HS list ya TBS inajumuisha vehicle parts/accessories pamoja na motorcycle parts. TBS pia imesisitiza quality compliance ya imported car parts kwa sababu ya usalama wa barabarani. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

15.4 PART NUMBER ENGINE
Hii ni canonical identity field.
PART NUMBER
├── Manufacturer Part Number
├── OEM Part Number
├── Supplier Part Number
├── Cross Reference Number
└── Alternate Part Number
Muhimu:
Part Number ≠ SKU
Mfano
Manufacturer Part Number
→ ABC-123

SokoHai SKU
→ SKH-PART-000182
SokoHai inaweza kuwa na SKU yake huku part number ya manufacturer ikibaki unchanged.
________________________________________
15.5 OEM VS AFTERMARKET
Classification:
PART SOURCE
├── Genuine OEM
├── OEM Equivalent
├── Aftermarket
├── Performance
├── Reconditioned
├── Remanufactured
└── Used
Hii isiwe category.
________________________________________
15.6 ENGINE PARTS
ENGINE PARTS

15.42 CROSS-REFERENCE ENGINE
Mfano:
OEM Part Number
       ↕
Aftermarket Part Number
       ↕
Supplier Part Number
Hii inasaidia seller/search:
“Nina part number ABC123”
Search engine inaweza kupata:
OEM
Aftermarket
Equivalent
Compatible
Lakini equivalent lazima iwe sourced/verified, si AI kuamua yenyewe.
________________________________________
15.43 OEM NUMBER ≠ COMPATIBILITY
Part number inaweza kuwa identity:
ABC123
Lakini compatibility ni relationship:
ABC123
→ Hilux 2018
→ Hilux 2019
→ Hilux 2020
Hivyo:
PART
na:
FITMENT
ni entities tofauti.
________________________________________
15.44 POSITION ≠ VARIANT
Mfano:
Brake Pad
Front Left
Front Right
Hatuwezi kuunda:
Variant = Left
Variant = Right
automatically.
Position ni fitment attribute.
Lakini kama manufacturer anauza:
SKU A = Front Left
SKU B = Front Right
ndipo SKU/variant identity inaweza kutokea.
________________________________________

Mfano:
Brake Pad Set
inaweza kuwa:
4 pieces
Hii si variant.
Ni:
Package Quantity

15.51 COMPLIANCE
Hapa ni muhimu kwa sababu TBS currently treats imported spare parts within its conformity procedures; Tanzania Trade Portal's spare-parts import procedure includes TBS verification of a Certificate of Conformity and physical verification of spare parts. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo:
COMPLIANCE
├── Applicable Standard
├── Standard Number
├── Certification
├── CoC
├── PVoC
├── Verification Status
└── Source
Lakini:
complianceStatus = "verified"
isije kwa sababu seller ameandika tu:
“Original / quality.”
________________________________________

Na sasa tuna engines 14 + 15 zinazoshirikiana vizuri

   │
   ├───────────────┐
   │               │
   ▼               ▼
CATEGORY 14      CATEGORY 15

   │               │

   └──── FITMENT ──┘
           │
           ▼
     COMPATIBILITY
Mfano halisi:
Toyota Hilux 2020
       │
       ├── Brake Pad
       ├── Oil Filter
       ├── Air Filter
       ├── Shock Absorber
       ├── Battery
       ├── Headlight
       └── Clutch Kit
Lakini hakuna data ya Hilux inayocopywa ndani ya kila spare part. Part inareference vehicle/fitment rules.
Hii ndiyo architecture itakayotuwezesha baadaye kufanya feature kali sana ya SokoHai:
“Chagua gari lako → SokoHai ikuonyeshe vipuri vinavyoendana nalo.”
Na pia reverse search:
“Nina Part Number ABC123 → ni magari gani inaendana nayo?”
TBS's current catalogue also lists standards around vehicle inspection/roadworthiness and automotive components, so this compliance/fitment layer is worth keeping separate from ordinary product attributes. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Category 15 imekamilika. inayofuata ni Category 16 — Usafiri & Logistics, ambapo tutaanza kuunganisha taxonomy ya bidhaa na engine ya SokoHai ya delivery, cargo, pickup, boda, scheduled, urgent, emergency, routes, agents, offers, acceptance, custody tokens na tracking bila kuunda engine mbili zinazofanya kazi moja.


Ndiyo. Tunaendelea, lakini tutafanya correction muhimu: kwa kuwa sasa tumeamua hii sehemu iwe BIDHAA TU, sitajaza Categories 17, 18, n.k. ambazo ni za Huduma. Tutaziruka kwa sasa na kuendelea na category inayofuata yenye bidhaa halisi.
Kwa hiyo sasa tunaingia:
CATEGORY 19 — AFYA & BIDHAA ZA AFYA
Hapa tunazungumzia vitu vinavyouzwa/kununuliwa, si huduma za daktari, hospitali, consultation, pharmacy service n.k.
________________________________________
19. AFYA & BIDHAA ZA AFYA
19.1 Dawa na Medicinal Products
Hii ni sehemu yenye compliance kubwa, hivyo taxonomy isiweke tu medicine.
Product Types:
•	Tablets
•	Capsules
•	Syrups
•	Suspensions
•	Solutions
•	Creams
•	Ointments
•	Gels
•	Drops
•	Injections
•	Suppositories
•	Powders
•	Granules
•	Medicated patches
•	Other dosage forms
Attributes:
•	Product name
•	Generic name
•	Brand
•	Active ingredient
•	Strength
•	Dosage form
•	Route of administration
•	Pack size
•	Unit
•	Manufacturer
•	Country of origin
•	Batch/Lot
•	Manufacturing date
•	Expiry date
•	Registration number
•	Prescription status
•	Storage condition
•	Temperature requirement
•	Packaging
•	Condition
Variant examples:
Paracetamol
 ├── 500 mg × 10 tablets
 ├── 500 mg × 20 tablets
 └── 500 mg × 100 tablets
Lakini:
500 mg
si lazima iwe variant; inaweza kuwa strength attribute.

Variant logic
Hatutaweka:
EVERY ATTRIBUTE = VARIANT
Badala yake:
ATTRIBUTE
   ↓
Does it change SKU/price/stock/order identity?
   ↓
YES → VARIANT
NO  → SPECIFICATION
Mfano:
Glucometer
Brand = XYZ                 → Attribute
Model = ABC                 → Identity
Measurement Range = ...     → Specification
Bluetooth = Yes             → Feature/Specification
Color = Black               → inaweza kuwa Variant
Gloves
Material = Nitrile         → Attribute
Powder-free = Yes          → Feature
Size = M                    → Variant
Size = L                    → Variant
100 pcs                     → Package/Selling Unit
Medicine
Active ingredient           → Attribute
Strength                    → Attribute
Dosage form                 → Attribute
Batch number                → Lot
Expiry                      → Lot
Pack size                   → Selling unit/possible Variant

Category 17/18 za Huduma tunaziruka kabisa kwa sasa. Hatutachanganya engines. Huduma tutarudi kuijenga kivyake baada ya kumaliza taxonomy ya bidhaa.


Hatua inayofuata: Category 27 — Sanaa & Ubunifu / bidhaa za sanaa, kisha 28, 29, 30, 31, 32... kwa mtindo huu huu wa kina.
Ndiyo. Tunaendelea BIDHAA TU. Na nimeangalia pia Tanzania Trade Portal kama reference ya upana wa bidhaa za Tanzania; portal ina categories kama textiles, furniture, glassware, cosmetics, medical devices, minerals, leather, timber, n.k., hivyo taxonomy ya SokoHai haitakiwi kuwa ya “online shop” pekee. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

Hapa tunamaanisha bidhaa zinazotengenezwa/kuuzwa kama vitu vya sanaa, ubunifu na creative goods.

Hii pia inaendana na ukweli kwamba Tanzania Trade Portal hutenganisha bidhaa kama furniture, glassware, textiles, leather na timber kama product/trade classifications; SokoHai itatumia taxonomy yake ya biashara juu yake bila ku-copy HS-code structure kama category tree yetu. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kanuni yetu kuu inaendelea kuwa:
Bidhaa moja iwe na identity moja, lakini inaweza kuwa na classifications/context/relationships nyingi bila ku-copy product yenyewe.
Kwa hiyo Category 27 imekamilika kwa msingi huo.
Inayofuata: Category 28 — Bidhaa za Jadi & Utamaduni, nayo tutaitengeneza kama bidhaa, si huduma wala “culture category” ya jumla.




Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na BIDHAA TU.
CATEGORY 28 — BIDHAA ZA JADI & UTAMADUNI
Hii ni category muhimu sana kwa SokoHai kwa sababu tunataka mfumo uweze kubeba bidhaa za Tanzania za jadi, asili, sanaa, urithi na matumizi ya kitamaduni, kutoka vijijini mpaka mijini.
Lakini kuna kanuni moja muhimu:
“Traditional” ni context ya bidhaa, si kila mara category yenyewe.
Kwa Category 28 tunaweka bidhaa ambazo utamaduni/urithi wa jadi ndiyo identity yake kuu.
Mfano:
•	Kitenge cha kawaida → Category 07
•	Kikapu cha kawaida cha matumizi → Category 09/27 kulingana na matumizi
•	Kikapu cha jadi cha kiutamaduni → Category 28
•	Mbao mbichi → Category 06
•	Kinyago cha kisanaa → Category 27
•	Kinyago cha jadi/heritage → Category 28
________________________________________

28.11 Traditional Hunting/Fishing Craft Objects

•	Traditional fishing equipment

28.12 Traditional Food Products
Hapa tunahitaji boundary kali kwa sababu Category 01 tayari ipo.
Mfano:
•	Traditional spices
•	Traditional flour
•	Traditional snacks
•	Traditional preserved foods
•	Traditional drinks
•	Indigenous food products
Rule
Product identity = Food
        ↓
Category 01
Traditional = TRUE kama context.
Kwa hiyo hatuta-copy bidhaa kutoka Category 01 kwenda Category 28.
Category 28 inabaki kwa non-food cultural products, isipokuwa bidhaa maalum za cultural craft ambazo identity yake ni heritage product.
________________________________________

28.23 Artisan / Maker Identity
Hii si subcategory; ni relationship.
PRODUCT
 ↓

•	Community group
•	Small business

28.24 Cultural Origin
Tunaweza kuwa na structured fields:
Country
Region
District
Locality
Cultural Region
Maker Origin
Production Location
Lakini:
Location ≠ Cultural Identity.
Bidhaa iliyotengenezwa Dar es Salaam inaweza kuwa inspired by a different cultural tradition.
Kwa hiyo hatusemi automatically:
Made in Dar es Salaam
=
Dar es Salaam traditional product

 ↓

 ↓
Color = Natural

SKU-002 → Medium
SKU-003 → Large
Traditional necklace
Material = Beads
Pattern = X
Length = 45cm

SKU / PRICE / STOCK / ORDER IDENTITY
inapotenganishwa.

CATEGORY 28 BOUNDARY YA MWISHO
RAW FOREST MATERIAL
→ 06

FOOD
→ 01
+ Traditional Context

FASHION
→ 07
+ Traditional Context

BEAUTY
→ 08
+ Traditional Context

HOME PRODUCT
→ 09
+ Traditional Context

ART
→ 27

TRADITIONAL CULTURAL PRODUCT
→ 28

RAW GEMSTONE / MINERAL
→ 43

SERVICE
→ HUDAUMA ENGINE
Kwa hiyo hatutengenezi duplicate database ya:
“Traditional Products.”
Badala yake Category 28 inabeba bidhaa ambazo cultural/heritage identity yake ndiyo msingi, huku bidhaa kutoka categories nyingine zikitumia context = TRADITIONAL pale inapohitajika.
Category 28 imekaa hivyo.
Inayofuata: CATEGORY 29 — HARUSI & SHEREHE, lakini tutaichambua kwa mtazamo wa bidhaa zinazouzwa tu: nguo, mapambo, vifaa vya sherehe, gifts, décor, wedding accessories, party supplies, n.k.; booking, event planning na huduma tutaziacha kabisa.




Ndiyo. Tunaendelea BIDHAA TU, na hapa tunafika:
CATEGORY 29 — HARUSI & SHEREHE
Hii category iwe ya vitu vinavyouzwa kwa ajili ya harusi, birthday, graduation, baby shower, Eid, Christmas, send-off, kitchen party, anniversary, events n.k.
Haijumuishi event planning, decorator service, photographer, DJ, MC, catering service, venue booking au makeup service — hizo ni Huduma baadaye.
Tanzania Trade Portal tayari inaonyesha kuwa bidhaa za biashara zinajumuisha textiles, furniture, electrical appliances, glassware, cosmetics na bidhaa nyingine nyingi; kwa hiyo hapa tunaunda shopping taxonomy ya SokoHai, si kuiga HS-code classification ya serikali. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

Kwa hiyo Category 29 si “kila kitu kinachotumika kwenye harusi.” Ni sehemu ya bidhaa za event + event context layer inayoweza kuhusisha bidhaa za categories nyingine.
Hii ndiyo itazuia taxonomy yetu kuwa na duplicates.
Category 29 imekamilika.
Inayofuata: Category 30 — Mama, Mtoto & Familia, tukibaki kwenye bidhaa pekee: maternity wear, baby clothes, feeding products, nursery products, diapers, strollers, car seats, toys zinazohusika, family-care products, n.k.



Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na BIDHAA TU. Kwa Category 30, tutatenganisha vizuri bidhaa za mama, mtoto na familia ili tusijenge category inayochanganya kila kitu cha nyumbani.
Pia kuna sababu ya kuweka compliance kwenye taxonomy kwa masharti: TBS ina kiwango cha lazima cha disposable baby diapers, na TBS inaendelea kuchapisha/kuhuisha viwango vya bidhaa; kwa hiyo mfumo uta-trigger compliance kulingana na product type badala ya kudhani kila bidhaa ya watoto ina requirement sawa. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 30 — MAMA, MTOTO & FAMILIA
MAMA, MTOTO & FAMILIA
│
├── Maternity & Pregnancy
├── Baby Clothing
├── Children's Clothing
├── Baby Diapers & Changing
├── Baby Feeding
├── Breastfeeding
├── Baby Bath & Hygiene
├── Baby Health Products
├── Nursery & Sleeping
├── Strollers & Mobility
├── Car Seats & Travel
├── Baby Carriers
├── Baby Safety
├── Baby Toys & Development
├── Baby Furniture
├── School/Child Accessories
├── Family Care Products
├── Maternity Accessories
├── Baby Gifts & Keepsakes
└── Family/Child Product Bundles
Tanzania Trade Portal pia inaorodhesha kwa ujumla bidhaa za textiles, furniture, electrical appliances, cosmetics na medical/veterinary products, kwa hiyo Category 30 itatumia context ya mama/mtoto/familia bila ku-duplicate bidhaa hizo kwenye database. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

CHILD / BABY / MATERNITY
Hivyo bidhaa moja inaweza kuonekana kwenye Discover ya “Mama & Mtoto” bila ku-copy product yenyewe.
Na kwa bidhaa zinazohitaji viwango, compliance inakuwa dynamic; kwa mfano TBS inaweka disposable baby diapers kwenye compulsory standards, huku standards nyingine zikiwa specific kwa product type. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Category 30 imekamilika.
Inayofuata: Category 31 — Wanyama & Pets, tukiendelea na bidhaa tu: pet food, cages, bedding, grooming products, accessories, aquariums, pet equipment, animal-care products n.k.; pet grooming/veterinary/boarding services tutaziacha kabisa.




Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na BIDHAA TU, na sasa:
CATEGORY 31 — WANYAMA & PETS
Hapa tunatenganisha vizuri mnyama mwenyewe na bidhaa za kumhudumia mnyama.
Tumefanya Category 04 — Mifugo kwa livestock kama ng'ombe, mbuzi, kondoo, kuku n.k. Category 31 inalenga zaidi pets, companion animals na bidhaa zinazohusiana nao.
Pia Tanzania Trade Portal inatambua Animal feeds kama product type tofauti, na TBS ina viwango vya compulsory vya baadhi ya animal feeds, ikiwemo compounded dog food, cat food, rabbit feed na hay. Hivyo animal-feed compliance itakuwa conditional kwenye product type husika. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

31.3 Dog Food
Specialized profile:
•	Dry food
•	Wet food
•	Canned food
•	Raw/frozen food where lawful
•	Puppy food
•	Adult food
•	Senior food
•	Breed-specific food
•	Treats
•	Dental chews
Compliance
TBS currently lists:
•	TZS 1983-2:2025/EAS 58-2:2023 — Compounded dog food — Part 2: Complementary food
•	and East African standards include compounded dog food specifications. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo:
Product Type = Dog Food
        ↓
Animal Feed Compliance Profile
ina-trigger.
Haimaanishi kila dog accessory ipate feed compliance.

Jambo muhimu zaidi
Tanzania Trade Portal inatambua animal feeds kama aina ya bidhaa ya biashara, na TBS kwa sasa ina compulsory standards mahsusi kwa baadhi ya feeds, ikiwa ni pamoja na dog food, cat food, rabbit feed na hay. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo SokoHai itafanya:
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
Does this product trigger a regulatory profile?
 ↓
YES → Compliance fields
NO  → Normal product flow
Sio:
Category 31
→ kila bidhaa lazima iwe na certification
Hilo ni muhimu sana kwa production system.
Category 31 imekamilika.
Inayofuata: Category 32 — Ofisi, Shule & Stationery, nayo tutaichambua kama bidhaa zinazouzwa: stationery, school supplies, office supplies, paper, writing instruments, filing/storage, printing consumables, educational materials, n.k.



Ndiyo. Tunaendelea Category 32 — Ofisi, Shule & Stationery, tukibaki kwenye BIDHAA ZINAZOUZWA/KUNUNULIWA TU.
TBS inaonyesha wazi kuwa stationery/paper products ni eneo lenye standards zake; kwenye PVoC HS list kuna registers, notebooks, exercise books, binders, folders, receipt books na bidhaa nyingine za stationery. Pia TBS ina technical work maalum ya Stationery and Paper Products. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

Muhimu sana
A4, A3, A5 si variants automatically.
Kama seller ana:
A4 80gsm → SKU A
A3 80gsm → SKU B
A4 100gsm → SKU C
na kila moja ina stock/bei tofauti, ndipo variant/SKU structure inatumika.

VARIANT RULES
Mfano 1 — Paper
A4 80gsm
A4 100gsm
A3 80gsm
Variant ikiwa SKU/price/stock zinatofautiana.
Mfano 2 — Pen
Blue
Black
Red
Variant kama stock/price/SKU tofauti.
Mfano 3 — Exercise Book
96 pages
120 pages
200 pages
Inaweza kuwa variant ikiwa manufacturer/seller anaitreat kama SKUs tofauti.
Mfano 4 — Printer toner
HP 12A
HP 85A
HP 106A
Hizi si variants za generic toner.
Ni different product identities/part numbers.

SKU
Category 32 itatumia central SKU engine:
PRODUCT
 ↓
VARIANT / CONFIGURATION
 ↓
SKU
 ↓
INVENTORY
Mfano:
Product:
A4 Copier Paper

Variant:
80 GSM / 500 Sheets

SKU:
PPR-A4-80-500

Inventory:
Store A → 40 reams
Store B → 15 reams
Lakini SKU format yenyewe isibuniwe kama engine ya Category 32. Itatumia central SKU authority.

piece

carton
roll
sheet
set
book
bundle
Lakini:
Unit ≠ Variant.
Mfano:
1 ream = 500 sheets
Hiyo ni packaging/selling unit.

Na si:
Office
 ├── separate paper engine
 ├── separate size engine
 ├── separate color engine
 ├── separate compatibility engine
 └── separate unit engine
Tunatumia central dictionaries/profiles tulizojenga kwenye categories zilizopita.
TBS pia inaeleza kuwa compulsory standards hutumika pale bidhaa zinapohusisha masuala kama afya, usalama, mazingira au athari kubwa kiuchumi; hivyo compliance ya Category 32 itakuwa conditional, si kila stationery item kuwekewa certification fields zote. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Category 32 imekamilika.
Inayofuata: Category 33 — Viwanda & Mashine — tutaingia kwenye industrial machinery, production equipment, generators/machinery boundaries, spare components, capacity/power, model/serial, used machinery, attachments na variant/SKU logic, bado BIDHAA TU.


Ndiyo. Sasa tunaingia Category 33 — Viwanda & Mashine, na hapa tunahitaji kuwa makini zaidi kwa sababu hii category inaweza kuwa kubwa sana. Tutabaki kwenye bidhaa/mashine zinazouzwa, si huduma za installation, repair, maintenance, fabrication au operator service.
TBS ina standards katika maeneo ya mechanical engineering, production, electrical engineering, energy na ICT, na catalogue yake ina zaidi ya standards 2,000 za hiari na lazima. Pia Tanzania Trade Portal inaeleza kwamba baadhi ya imported machinery inaweza kufanyiwa inspection badala ya testing, kulingana na bidhaa/procedure. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 33 — VIWANDA & MASHINE

33.3 Machine Identity — MUHIMU SANA
Kwa mashine kubwa:
MACHINE PRODUCT
       ↓
BRAND
       ↓
MODEL
       ↓
CONFIGURATION
       ↓
VARIANT
       ↓
SKU
       ↓
PHYSICAL MACHINE
       ↓
SERIAL NUMBER / ASSET ID
Usichanganye:
Model ≠ Variant ≠ Physical Unit
Mfano:
CNC Machine X100
        ↓
Model = X100

Configuration:
380V / 10kW

Variant:
10kW / 15kW

Physical Unit:
Serial No. SN-84721
Serial number ni ya machine halisi, si variant.

= USED

→ Category 33

→ 2021

→ 4,800 h

33.33 Attachments

 ↓

•	Fork attachment

•	Conveyor attachment
•	Blades

Ni related product/component.

33.34 Machine Compatibility
Hii ndiyo relationship muhimu:
COMPONENT
       ↓
Compatible With
       ↓
Machine Brand
       ↓
Machine Model
       ↓
Model Year / Generation
       ↓
Configuration
Mfano:
Hydraulic pump P-200
→ Compatible with
→ Machine X
→ Model X200
→ Configuration C
Compatibility ≠ attribute ya kawaida.
Ni relationship ya entity.
________________________________________
33.35 Industrial Safety Profile
Kwa machine fulani:

Hii iwe conditional.
TBS inaeleza kuwa compulsory standards hutumika hasa pale ambapo afya, usalama, mazingira au athari kubwa kwa uchumi zinausika. Kwa hiyo hatutaki kuweka “certified” kama field inayodaiwa kwa kila mashine. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

33.40 Variant Rules
Hapa tusifanye kosa la kutengeneza variants nyingi.
Example
Industrial Pump
Specifications:
Flow = 100 L/min
Pressure = 6 bar
Power = 3kW
Hizi si variants lazima.
Lakini:
Pump X
 ├── 2kW → SKU A
 ├── 3kW → SKU B
 └── 5kW → SKU C
kama manufacturer/seller anazitenganisha kama SKUs zenye inventory/price tofauti → variants.
________________________________________
33.41 SKU
PRODUCT
 ↓
CONFIGURATION
 ↓
VARIANT
 ↓
SKU
 ↓
INVENTORY
Mfano:
Product:
Industrial Water Pump

Variant:
5HP / 3-phase

SKU:
IWP-5HP-3P
Lakini SKU generator iwe central, sio Category 33-specific.
________________________________________
33.42 PHYSICAL MACHINE
Hapa tunahitaji distinction kubwa:
PRODUCT MODEL
      ↓
SKU
      ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
      ↓
SERIAL NUMBER
      ↓
ASSET RECORD
Mfano:
Model: ABC-500
SKU: ABC500-10KW
Serial: SN-2026-00471
Inventory inaweza kusema:
Quantity = 1
lakini physical unit ina identity yake.
Hii itakuwa muhimu sana kwa:
•	used machinery
•	warranty
•	repairs
•	ownership transfer
•	service history
•	asset tracking
Lakini service history tutaiunganisha baadaye kwenye Huduma/Management, si kuijenga sasa ndani ya product taxonomy.
________________________________________
33.43 BUSINESS BOUNDARY
Industrial machine
→ 33

Agricultural machine
→ 02
unless industrial processing

Food-processing machine
→ 33

Household blender
→ 11

Industrial mixer
→ 33

Car
→ 14

Car spare part
→ 15

Machine spare/component
→ 33 / shared component authority

Construction machine
→ 10 or 33 depending primary product classification/context

Generator
→ 34 when energy-generation equipment
or relevant equipment classification

Solar equipment
→ 34

Office printer
→ 11

Industrial printer
→ 33

Laptop controlling a machine
→ 13

Machine installation
→ Huduma later

Machine repair
→ Huduma later

Machine operator
→ Huduma later

Machine rental
→ separate transaction/service model later
________________________________________
33.44 COMPLIANCE
Hatutaweka:
Category 33 = certified
Badala yake:
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
 ↓
IF REQUIRED
    → Standard
    → Certificate
    → Inspection
    → Conformity
    → Import documentation
Tanzania Trade Portal inaonyesha kwamba kwa baadhi ya machinery, minerals na raw materials, inspection inaweza kutumika badala ya testing katika taratibu husika; kwa imported goods, conformity documentation inaweza pia kuhitajika kulingana na procedure/product. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo SokoHai isiweke sheria ya jumla kwamba:
“Mashine zote lazima ziwe na TBS certificate.”
Badala yake taxonomy ita-trigger conditional compliance kulingana na product type na applicable rules.
________________________________________
33.45 AI PRODUCT-FILL BOT
Hapa Category 33 ni sehemu nzuri sana ya kutumia AI.
Seller anaandika:
“Nauza mashine ya kusaga mahindi 10HP, 3 phase, uwezo 500kg kwa saa, mpya.”
AI inaweza kupendekeza:
Category
→ 33 Viwanda & Mashine
Product Type
→ Grain Milling Machine
Application
→ Maize Processing
Power
→ 10 HP
Phase
→ 3 Phase
Capacity
→ 500 kg/hour
Lakini AI haitakiwi kubuni:
Brand
Model
Certification
Serial Number
Safety certification
Exact voltage
Warranty
ikiwa seller hajatoa taarifa hizo.

33.46 FINAL MASTER MODEL
Kwa Category 33 tunapata:
CATEGORY
   ↓
SUBCATEGORY
   ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
   ↓
MACHINE PROFILE
   ↓
SPECIFICATIONS
   ↓
CAPACITY / POWER / UNIT
   ↓
COMPATIBILITY
   ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
   ↓
VARIANT RULE
   ↓
VARIANT
   ↓
SKU
   ↓
INVENTORY
   ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
   ↓
SERIAL / ASSET ID
Na hii haiundi engines mpya kwa:
•	Units
•	Power
•	Compatibility
•	Condition
•	Warranty
•	SKU
•	Inventory
•	Compliance
Zote zinasoma kutoka kwenye central authorities tulizokwisha kubuni.
Category 33 imekamilika.
Category inayofuata ni 34 — Nishati & Umeme, ambayo itahitaji kutenganisha vizuri solar, generators, batteries, inverters, electrical distribution, cables, lighting, renewable-energy equipment na power systems bila kurudia Category 11 Electronics, Category 12 Communication, au Category 10 Electrical Installation Materials.



Ndiyo. Sasa tunaingia CATEGORY 34 — NISHATI & UMEME. Hii ni category ambayo tunahitaji kuijenga kwa umakini kwa sababu inagusa solar, batteries, inverters, generators, electrical distribution, lighting na energy-storage systems, lakini hatutaki kurudia Electronics, Hardware au Appliances.
TBS kwa sasa ina standards zinazohusiana moja kwa moja na renewable-energy storage, PV arrays, PV modules, batteries, UPS na EV charging; kwa mfano catalogue ya TBS ina TZS 1911-2:2025 kwa batteries za renewable-energy storage, TZS 2728:2021 kwa PV arrays, na TZS 3187-1:2023 kwa PV module safety. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 34 — NISHATI & UMEME

34.38 Product vs System vs Kit
Hapa kuna distinction muhimu sana:
Product
Solar Panel 550W
Variant
550W / Black Frame
Kit
Solar Home Kit
├── 2 panels
├── Battery
├── Controller
├── Lights
└── Cables
System
50kW Solar Energy System
├── PV array
├── Inverters
├── Battery
├── Protection
├── Monitoring
└── Mounting
System inaweza kuwa configured/custom order.

34.42 VARIANT RULES
Solar panel
450W
550W
600W
Variant if SKU differs.
Battery
12V 100Ah
12V 200Ah
24V 100Ah
Can be variants.
LED bulb
9W
12W
15W
Can be variants.
Cable
1.5mm²
2.5mm²
4mm²
6mm²
Can be variants.
Generator
3kVA
5kVA
10kVA
Often different models/products rather than variants. The classification engine should decide based on manufacturer model identity, not simply power.
Hii ni muhimu:
Model identity takes precedence over a generic variant assumption.

 ↓

 ↓

34.45 COMPLIANCE
Category 34 inahitaji conditional compliance, si blanket certification.
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
 ├── Applicable Standard
 ├── Certification
 ├── Testing
 ├── Inspection
 ├── Conformity
 ├── Import Documentation
 └── Verification Status
Mfano:
PV Module
→ PV-specific compliance profile

Battery for renewable storage
→ battery-specific profile

UPS
→ UPS safety profile

EV charger
→ EV charging profile
TBS's current compulsory-standard notice includes renewable-energy storage batteries, UPS safety, EV charging components and other electrical products. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Na Tanzania Trade Portal ina Electrical appliance kama product category kwenye trade procedures, pamoja na product areas nyingine; hivyo compliance/procedure haipaswi ku-hard-code kwenye UI ya kila electrical product. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

34.47 Jambo muhimu: Category 10 vs 34
Tumeshawahi kuweka Electrical Installation Materials ndani ya Category 10 — Ujenzi & Hardware.
Kwa hiyo tunahitaji rule hii:
Category 10
BUILDING ELECTRICAL INSTALLATION
├── Conduits
├── Installation accessories
├── Building wiring accessories
├── Electrical boxes
└── Construction-use installation products
Category 34
ENERGY & POWER PRODUCT
├── Solar
├── Batteries
├── Generators
├── Inverters
├── UPS
├── Transformers
├── Distribution
├── Electrical protection
├── Lighting
└── Energy monitoring
Kama product inaweza kuwa shared, canonical product inaweza kuwa Category moja na context/use/application ikaonyesha nyingine.
________________________________________

34.49 FINAL CANONICAL FLOW
Category 34 sasa inafuata:
CATEGORY
  ↓
SUBCATEGORY
  ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
  ↓
PROFILE
  ↓
ATTRIBUTES
  ↓
OPTIONS
  ↓
FILTERS
  ↓
FEATURES
  ↓
COMPATIBILITY
  ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
  ↓
VARIANT RULE
  ↓
VARIANT
  ↓
SKU
  ↓
INVENTORY
  ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
  ↓
SERIAL / ASSET
Na tunashikilia principle yetu:
Si kila tofauti ni Variant.
Kwa mfano:
550W
→ inaweza kuwa variant

22.5% efficiency
→ specification

Serial number
→ physical unit

MC4 connector
→ attribute/component relationship

50V Voc
→ specification

10kWh battery system
→ system configuration

Solar kit
→ bundle

Certification
→ compliance metadata
Category 34 imekamilika.
Category inayofuata ni 35 — Maji, Usafi & Mazingira. Huko tutatenganisha vizuri water storage, tanks, pipes/accessories, filtration, purification, sanitation products, cleaning/environmental equipment, waste-management products na water-treatment equipment, bila kurudia Category 10, Category 19, au Category 33.


Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na CATEGORY 35 — MAJI, USAFI & MAZINGIRA, tukibaki kwenye BIDHAA TU. Huduma kama water delivery, septic-tank emptying, garbage collection, cleaning service, plumbing service n.k. tutaacha kabisa kwa sehemu ya Huduma.
Kwa Tanzania, hii category inapaswa pia kuwa na conditional compliance layer, kwa sababu bidhaa za maji, sanitation na mazingira zinaweza kuwa chini ya standards/procedures tofauti kulingana na product type. Tanzania Trade Portal ina mfumo wa bidhaa, HS codes, procedures, laws/norms na conformity procedures, hivyo SokoHai haitakiwi ku-hard-code requirement moja kwa kila bidhaa. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

35.32 Eco-Friendly Products
Hapa tunahitaji kuwa makini sana.
“Eco-friendly” si product category identity kwa kila bidhaa.
Ni:
CONTEXT / CLAIM
→ Eco-oriented
Mfano:
Reusable water bottle
→ canonical product
+ Reusable = Feature

Compostable bag
→ canonical packaging/waste product
+ Compostable = attribute/verified claim
AI isiruhusiwe kuweka Eco-friendly = true kwa sababu tu product inaonekana natural.
________________________________________

35.42 VARIANT RULES
Water tank
Tank
├── 500L
├── 1,000L
├── 2,000L
└── 5,000L
Inaweza kuwa variants kama kila capacity ina SKU/stock/price tofauti.
Pipe
PVC Pipe
├── 20mm
├── 25mm
├── 32mm
Variant/SKU.
Filter cartridge
Filter
├── 5 micron
├── 10 micron
├── 20 micron
Inaweza kuwa variant ikiwa manufacturer/SKU structure inaitenganisha.
Cleaning chemical
Cleaner
├── 500ml
├── 1L
├── 5L
Pack size inaweza kuwa variant.
________________________________________
35.43 PRODUCT ≠ CONSUMABLE ≠ SYSTEM
Mfano:
Water Filter
→ Product

Replacement Cartridge
→ Product

Whole-house filtration system
→ System

5 replacement cartridges
→ Bundle/pack
Hii inazuia variants zisizo na maana.
________________________________________
35.44 INVENTORY
Central inventory engine ile ile:
PRODUCT
 ↓
SKU
 ↓
STORE
 ↓
INVENTORY
Examples:
1 tank
20 filter cartridges
300m pipe
50 cleaning bottles
Kwa pipe inayouzwa kwa mita:
Unit = metre
Kwa chemical:
Unit = litre
Kwa tank:
Unit = piece
Unit inatoka central Unit Dictionary.
________________________________________
35.45 PHYSICAL UNIT
Kwa products kama:
•	water tanks kubwa
•	purification systems
•	industrial water treatment units
•	monitoring equipment
•	pumps
•	large environmental equipment
tunaweza kuwa na:
SKU
 ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
 ↓
Serial Number
 ↓
Asset
Kwa consumable kama:
soap
filter cartridge
cleaning cloth
hakuna haja ya physical-unit identity.
________________________________________
35.46 COMPLIANCE
Hapa tunatumia:
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
 ↓
IF APPLICABLE
 ├── Standard
 ├── Certification
 ├── Testing
 ├── Inspection
 ├── Conformity
 ├── Import Requirement
 └── Documentation
Tanzania Trade Portal yenyewe inaonyesha tofauti kati ya product database, procedures, laws/norms na conformity-related procedures; hii inasaidia architectural decision ya kutenganisha Product Taxonomy na Compliance Authority. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo:
CATEGORY 35
≠
COMPLIANCE ENGINE
Category 35 inatick rule inayohitajika, compliance engine ndiyo inayoshughulikia requirement.

35.49 FINAL CANONICAL FLOW
CATEGORY
  ↓
SUBCATEGORY
  ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
  ↓
PRODUCT PROFILE
  ↓
ATTRIBUTES
  ↓
OPTIONS
  ↓
FILTERS
  ↓
FEATURES
  ↓
COMPATIBILITY
  ↓
COMPLIANCE RULE
  ↓
VARIANT RULE
  ↓
VARIANT
  ↓
SKU
  ↓
INVENTORY
  ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
Na bado tunafuata sheria yetu kuu:
Category 35 haitengenezi engine mpya kwa Units, Compatibility, Inventory, Compliance, SKU, Condition au Media. Inatumia authorities za central system.
Category 35 imekamilika.
Inayofuata ni Category 36 — Usalama, tukibaki kwenye bidhaa: CCTV, alarms, locks, safes, access-control devices, security lighting, personal protective/security products na related equipment—lakini tutaweka mipaka kali kati ya security products, electronics, communication devices, PPE, na Huduma za Ulinzi.




Ndiyo. Tunaendelea na CATEGORY 36 — USALAMA, na kama tulivyokubaliana, tunabaki PRODUCTS ONLY. Huduma za ulinzi, security guards, installation, monitoring-as-a-service, patrol n.k. zitaingia baadaye kwenye Huduma.
Nimeangalia pia vyanzo vya sasa vya Tanzania: TBS ina standards za CCTV/video-surveillance, alarm systems na electronic access control, huku standard ya Tanzania ya padlocks ikihusisha strength, security, durability na corrosion. TBS pia inaeleza kuwa baadhi ya standards huwa compulsory pale bidhaa inapohusu afya, usalama, mazingira au athari kubwa kiuchumi. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 36 — USALAMA

Kwa hiyo security grade inapaswa kuwa structured field inayoweza kujazwa kutoka kwenye specification ya bidhaa—AI isiibuni. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

36.35 PRODUCT ≠ SYSTEM
Hii ni muhimu sana kwenye security.
Camera
→ Product

NVR
→ Product

HDD
→ Product

PoE switch
→ Product

8-camera CCTV package
→ Bundle/System
Usitengeneze:
"CCTV Variant"
kwa kila combination ya:
8 cameras
+ NVR
+ HDD
+ cable
+ monitor
Huo ni configured bundle/system.
