36.38 PHYSICAL UNIT
Security products nyingi zinaweza kuwa na physical identity:
CAMERA
→ Serial Number

NVR
→ Serial Number

ACCESS CONTROLLER
→ Serial Number

GPS TRACKER
→ IMEI/Device ID

BIOMETRIC READER
→ Serial Number
Lakini:
Padlock
→ usually quantity inventory
unless seller needs individual asset tracking.
________________________________________
36.39 COMPLIANCE
Hapa tutafuata architecture yetu ile ile:
PRODUCT
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
CONDITIONAL COMPLIANCE RULE
 ↓
IF APPLICABLE
 ├── Standard
 ├── Certification
 ├── Test
 ├── Approval
 ├── Documentation
 └── Verification
TBS ina standards za CCTV/video surveillance, alarm systems na electronic access control, lakini hii haimaanishi kwamba kila product kwenye Category 36 inapaswa kupewa TBS-certified automatically. Requirement lazima itokane na product type na applicable rule. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Tanzania Trade Portal pia hutenganisha product information na procedures/laws/norms, hivyo SokoHai itafanya vivyo hivyo badala ya kuchanganya compliance ndani ya taxonomy. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

→ Usalama

Subcategory
→ CCTV & Video Surveillance

Product Type
→ IP Security Camera

→ 5MP

Night Vision
→ Yes

Night Vision Distance
→ 30m

→ IP66

→ Yes

Condition
→ New

Model
TBS Certification
AI Person Detection
8x Optical Zoom
Warranty
isipokuwa seller/manufacturer/source ametoa taarifa hizo.

36.43 Final Canonical Flow
CATEGORY
  ↓
SUBCATEGORY
  ↓

  ↓

  ↓

  ↓

  ↓

  ↓

  ↓
COMPATIBILITY
  ↓

  ↓
VARIANT RULE
  ↓
VARIANT
  ↓
SKU
  ↓
INVENTORY
  ↓
PHYSICAL UNIT
Na hapa kuna kanuni moja muhimu sana:
Security function ndiyo inayoweza kuleta product kwenye Category 36; hatutengenezi duplicate ya Electronics, Connectivity, GPS, Power, Lighting, Inventory au Compatibility engines.
TBS yenyewe inaonyesha kuwa security ni domain yenye standards tofauti—CCTV, alarms, access control na physical security products kama padlocks—kwa hiyo taxonomy yetu inaweza kuwa pana, lakini authority za technical specifications zibaki shared/central. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
CATEGORY 36 — USALAMA: IMEKAMILIKA.
Inayofuata ni CATEGORY 37 — Marketing, Media & Branding, tukibaki kwenye bidhaa zinazouzwa: printing materials, banners, signage, promotional merchandise, packaging/branding materials, display products, photography/video equipment products, media equipment n.k.—bila kuingiza huduma kama graphic design, photography service, advertising service au social-media management.


Ndiyo. Tunaingia sasa kwenye CATEGORY 37 — MARKETING, MEDIA & BRANDING, na bado tunafuata kanuni yetu: bidhaa tu, si huduma.
Hii category ni muhimu sana kwa SokoHai kwa sababu inahusisha vitu ambavyo biashara hununua ili kutangaza, kuweka brand, ku-display, kufungasha, ku-identify au ku-present bidhaa zao. Lakini hatutaki ku-copy categories nyingine: printer si Category 37, computer si 37, camera ya kawaida si 37, packaging ya chakula inaweza kuwa Category 01/32/09 kulingana na matumizi; hapa tunatumia marketing/branding purpose kama classification/context inapofaa.
Pia TBS ina guidelines maalum kuhusu promotional materials kwa food/cosmetics, na inaeleza kuwa objective claims/comparisons katika matangazo zinapaswa kuwa substantiated. Hii inaonyesha kwa nini Advertising/Claim Compliance inapaswa kuwa layer tofauti na Product Taxonomy. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)

37.36 Customization Profile

→ T-shirt

VARIANT

CONFIGURATION
→ Logo + Name + Placement
Hapo system inakuwa safi sana.

37.39 VARIANT RULES
Business card
SIZE × PAPER TYPE × FINISH
only if seller treats each combination as SKU.
T-shirt
SIZE × COLOR
using Category 07's clothing engine.
Mug
CAPACITY × COLOR
if separately stocked.
Banner
MATERIAL × SIZE
often better as configuration than massive variant matrix.
Sticker
MATERIAL × SIZE × FINISH
may be configuration.
Rule:
Custom print dimensions should default to configuration/quote, not thousands of variants.
________________________________________
37.40 PRODUCT VS CONFIGURATION VS SERVICE
Mfano:
PRINTED BANNER
→ Product

CUSTOM BANNER 3m × 1m
→ Product + Configuration

DESIGNING THE BANNER
→ Service

PRINTING THE BANNER
→ Could be service OR physical product workflow depending seller model
SokoHai itaruhusu seller kuwa na:
PRODUCT
+
SERVICE
lakini engines zisitengenezwe moja.

QUANTITY INVENTORY
si physical unit.
________________________________________
37.46 COMPLIANCE & CLAIMS
Hii category ina nuance kubwa kwa sababu advertising itself can contain claims.
TBS guidelines for food/cosmetic promotional materials say factual descriptions, claims and comparisons should be capable of substantiation, and promotional material should not mislead consumers. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
Kwa hiyo SokoHai inaweza kuwa na:
CLAIM
├── Claim Text
├── Claim Type
├── Evidence
├── Source
├── Verified
└── Applicable Product
Lakini:
Claim Engine ≠ Product Taxonomy.
Mfano seller akiandika:
“Hii cream inaondoa acne ndani ya siku 3.”
AI isiibadilishe kuwa:
Feature:
Acne removal in 3 days
Badala yake:
CLAIM
→ Seller-provided claim

Verification
→ pending / supported / restricted
Hii ni muhimu sana kwa sababu TBS ina mfumo wa kudhibiti promotional materials na guidelines zake zinahitaji objective claims ziwe substantiable. (Tanzania Bureau of Statistics)
________________________________________
37.47 COMPLIANCE STRUCTURE
PRODUCT
 ↓
CLAIM / PROMOTIONAL MATERIAL
 ↓
IF REGULATED
 ├── Applicable Rule
 ├── Evidence
 ├── Supporting Document
 ├── Approval/Certification
 └── Verification Status
Na usiweke TBS Certified automatically.
TBS inaeleza kuwa certification/testing ni process yenye application, sample evaluation/testing na certification outcome; pia Trade Portal ina procedures tofauti kwa product certification na import conformity. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
________________________________________

Marketing purpose haipaswi kutengeneza duplicate ya product iliyopo.
Kwa hiyo SokoHai inaweza kuelewa:

→ Category 07

→ Context: Promotional

→ Context: Promotional

→ Category 32

→ Category 32
→ Context: Promotional
Hii ndiyo itafanya Discover iwe kubwa bila database kujaa duplicate products.
CATEGORY 37 — IMEKAMILIKA.
Kinachofuata ni CATEGORY 38 — Digital Products & Online Services. Hapo tutahitaji kuwa makini zaidi kutenganisha digital product, software/license, digital content, subscription, account-based product, downloadable file, online service, na Huduma, ili tusichanganye bidhaa ya kidigitali na huduma.




Ndiyo. Sasa tunaingia CATEGORY 38 — DIGITAL PRODUCTS & ONLINE SERVICES.
Hii ndiyo category ambayo inahitaji separation kali zaidi kati ya:
DIGITAL PRODUCT
≠
SOFTWARE
≠
DIGITAL CONTENT
≠
LICENSE
≠
SUBSCRIPTION
≠
ONLINE SERVICE
≠
PHYSICAL PRODUCT
Na muhimu zaidi kwa SokoHai: Huduma za kidigitali zisitengeneze duplicate ya service engine ya Huduma. Tunachotengeneza hapa ni taxonomy ya bidhaa za kidigitali na digital-delivery objects, huku huduma za kufanyiwa na mtu zikipelekwa kwenye Huduma.
Kwa Tanzania, hii pia ni eneo lenye regulatory considerations: TCRA inaendelea kuwa na framework ya electronic communications/online content, na 2026 kulikuwa na amendment ya Online Content Regulations iliyoanza kutumika 1 Julai 2026. Pia Personal Data Protection Act inahusu collection, processing, storage, disclosure na transfer ya personal data; PDPC ilitangaza full enforcement kuanzia 9 Aprili 2026. (TCRA)
________________________________________

Very important
Personal Data
→ special privacy/compliance handling
Tanzania's Personal Data Protection Act applies to collection, processing, storage, disclosure and transfer of personal data, and PDPC says digital platforms/e-commerce businesses are among sectors subject to its enforcement framework. (PDPC)
Kwa hiyo seller asiweze kuuza:
“Database ya namba za simu za watu Dar es Salaam”
kama ordinary digital product bila appropriate legal/privacy handling.

38.41 Digital Product vs Service
Hii ndiyo boundary muhimu zaidi:
id="digital-vs-service"

→ kitu kinachoweza kutolewa/entitle/download/access kama product

→ kazi/utekelezaji unaofanywa kwa mteja
Examples
E-book
→ DIGITAL PRODUCT

Excel template
→ DIGITAL PRODUCT

Website theme
→ DIGITAL PRODUCT

API subscription
→ DIGITAL PRODUCT

→ DIGITAL PRODUCT

Graphic design
→ HUDAUMA

Website development
→ HUDAUMA

→ HUDAUMA

→ HUDAUMA

Online tutoring session
→ HUDAUMA

Digital marketing campaign management
→ HUDAUMA

38.45 Privacy & Personal Data Layer
Kwa Category 38 hii ni muhimu sana.
DIGITAL PRODUCT
 ↓
DATA HANDLING PROFILE
 ├── Collects Personal Data?
 ├── Processes Personal Data?
 ├── Stores Personal Data?
 ├── Transfers Personal Data?
 ├── Data Location
 ├── Retention
 ├── User Rights
 └── Privacy Documentation
Hii si attribute ya kawaida ya Discover.
Ni compliance/security metadata.
PDPC inaeleza kwamba data protection framework inahusu controllers na processors, na tovuti yake ya sasa inaeleza registration ya data controllers/processors pamoja na principles kama lawfulness, purpose limitation, data minimization na storage limitation. (PDPC)
________________________________________
38.46 Online Content / Media Layer
Kwa digital products zinazokuwa online media/content, system inahitaji compliance hook tofauti na ordinary downloadable templates/software.
DIGITAL PRODUCT
 ↓
CONTENT TYPE
 ↓
IF ONLINE MEDIA
 ↓
REGULATORY CHECK
TCRA ilichapisha Electronic and Postal Communications (Online Content) (Amendment) Regulations, 2026, zilizotajwa kuanza kutumika 1 Julai 2026. Kwa hiyo hatupaswi hard-code kwamba kila digital product ni “online media”; tunatengeneza conditional regulatory classification. (TCRA)
________________________________________
38.47 Digital Product Compliance
PRODUCT
 ↓
DIGITAL TYPE
 ↓
IF APPLICABLE
 ├── License
 ├── Copyright/usage rights
 ├── Data protection
 ├── Online-content requirements
 ├── Age/content restrictions
 ├── Platform requirements
 └── Consumer/e-commerce requirements
SokoHai isiwe inatoa legal verdict automatically.
Badala yake:
Compliance Status
→ Not Applicable
→ Required
→ Pending
→ Seller Declared
→ Verified
→ Restricted

Kanuni kuu ya Category 38
Digital product ni kitu kinachouzwa kama digital asset, license, access, entitlement au standardized subscription; kazi inayofanywa kwa mteja ibaki Huduma.
Na architecture yake inapaswa kuunganishwa na existing:
Product
→ SKU
→ Order
→ SokoPay
→ Fulfillment
→ Digital Delivery
badala ya kutengeneza DigitalCommerceEngine tofauti.
CATEGORY 38 — IMEKAMILIKA.
Kinachofuata ni CATEGORY 39 — Used, Second-hand & Refurbished. Hapo tutafanya correction muhimu: kwa architecture tuliyokubaliana, 39 si lazima iwe product category halisi; New/Used/Refurbished mara nyingi ni Condition/Context. Kwa hiyo tutaamua kwa usahihi kama 39 ibaki kama discovery/business classification layer badala ya duplicate ya bidhaa zote.

Nimekuelewa. Tatizo ni kwamba nilichanganya “Category 39” na “Condition Engine”. Tuiweke sawa kabisa bila kuacha nafasi ya mkanganyiko.
CATEGORY 39 — USED, SECOND-HAND & REFURBISHED
Category 39 ibaki kwenye Category Master kama category ya Discover, lakini isiwe na taxonomy yake ya bidhaa inayoduplicate Categories 01–38.
Maana yake:
CATEGORY 39
Used, Second-hand & Refurbished
inawakilisha bidhaa zilizopo kwenye market ambazo hali yake ni used/second-hand/refurbished, kutoka kwenye categories zao halisi.
Mfano
Used iPhone
→ Category 12: Simu & Mawasiliano
→ Category 39: Used, Second-hand & Refurbished
→ Condition: Used
Refurbished Laptop
→ Category 13: Computers & IT
→ Category 39: Used, Second-hand & Refurbished
→ Condition: Refurbished
Used Sofa
→ Category 09: Nyumba & Samani
→ Category 39: Used, Second-hand & Refurbished
→ Condition: Used
Kwa hiyo Category 39 ni cross-category discovery category, si category inayotengeneza “Used Phones / Used Laptops / Used Sofas” tena.
________________________________________
STRUCTURE YA CATEGORY 39
Tuiandike rasmi hivi:
39. USED, SECOND-HAND & REFURBISHED
│
├── 1. Used Products
│
├── 2. Second-hand Products
│
├── 3. Refurbished Products
│
├── 4. Open-box Products
│
├── 5. Reconditioned Products
│
├── 6. Repaired Products
│
├── 7. For Parts
│
├── 8. Salvaged Products
│
└── 9. Damaged Products
Hizi ni discovery classifications, si product subcategories za kawaida.

•	Used appliance

39.9 DAMAGED
Damaged Products

HII NDIYO RELATIONSHIP SAHIHI
                  CATEGORY MASTER
                       │
          ┌────────────┴────────────┐
          ↓                         ↓

Kwa hiyo Category 39 haisimamii Product identity.

________________________________________
Naomba tuiweke hivi kwenye master document
39. USED, SECOND-HAND & REFURBISHED

TYPE:
Cross-Category Discovery / Market Classification

PURPOSE:
Kukusanya na kugundua bidhaa kutoka categories mbalimbali
ambazo condition yake ni Used, Second-hand, Refurbished,
Open-box, Reconditioned, Repaired, For Parts, Salvaged au Damaged.

SUBCATEGORIES / DISCOVERY TYPES:
1. Used Products
2. Second-hand Products
3. Refurbished Products
4. Open-box Products
5. Reconditioned Products
6. Repaired Products
7. For Parts
8. Salvaged Products
9. Damaged Products

NOTES:
- Haifanyi duplicate ya Product Taxonomy.
- Product lazima ibaki na Primary Category yake halisi.
- Category 39 inatumika kama cross-category discovery classification.
- Condition Engine ndiyo canonical authority ya condition.
- Used/Refurbished si Variant kwa default.
- Physical used item inaweza kuwa na Physical Unit/Serial/IMEI/VIN/Asset ID.
- Refurbishment details ziwe kwenye Refurbishment Profile.
- Repair history iwe kwenye Repair Profile.
- Warranty iwe kwenye Warranty Profile.
- Authenticity/verification isiundwe na AI bila evidence.
Hapo sasa Category 39 iko sawa: ni Category kwenye Master kwa ajili ya Discovery, lakini Condition ndiyo authority ya data ya bidhaa.



Ndiyo. Sasa tunaenda CATEGORY 40 — WHOLESALE & BULK TRADE.
Hapa pia kuna correction muhimu kama ya 39: Wholesale/Bulk si aina ya bidhaa; ni commercial/market mode. Lakini kwa kuwa tumeweka 40 kwenye Category Master, tuipe nafasi ya cross-category discovery classification bila ku-duplicate bidhaa.
CATEGORY 40 — WHOLESALE & BULK TRADE
40. WHOLESALE & BULK TRADE

TYPE:
Cross-Category Discovery / Commercial Classification
40.1 Muundo

│

Hizi si product subcategories za kawaida. Ni njia za ku-discover na ku-classify bidhaa zinazouzwa kwa biashara, kwa quantity kubwa, au kwa trade units.

isipokuwa seller ametoa taarifa hizo.

40.22 PRODUCT + WHOLESALE
Mfumo mzima:
CATEGORY
 ↓
SUBCATEGORY
 ↓
PRODUCT TYPE
 ↓
ATTRIBUTES
 ↓
OPTIONS
 ↓
FILTERS
 ↓
FEATURES
 ↓
CONDITION
 ↓
MARKET CONTEXT
 ↓
COMMERCIAL MODE
 ↓
TRADE UNIT
 ↓
MOQ
 ↓
PRICING RULE
 ↓
VARIANT
 ↓
SKU
 ↓
INVENTORY
Hii inafanya Categories 01–38 ziendelee kuwa source of truth.
________________________________________
40.23 Category 39 + 40 Zinaweza Kufanya Kazi Pamoja
Mfano wa mitumba:
Category 07
→ Nguo

Condition
→ Second-hand

Category 39
→ Used/Second-hand Discovery

Commercial Mode
→ Wholesale

Category 40
→ Wholesale/Bulk Discovery

Trade Unit
→ Bale

MOQ
→ 10 bales
Hakuna duplicate product.
________________________________________
FINAL DEFINITION YA CATEGORY 40
40. WHOLESALE & BULK TRADE

TYPE:
Cross-Category Discovery / Commercial Classification

DISCOVERY TYPES:
1. Wholesale Products
2. Bulk Products
3. Wholesale Lots
4. Bulk Commodities
5. Case / Carton Trade
6. Box / Pack Trade
7. Bundle / Bale Trade
8. Pallet Trade
9. Sack / Bag Trade
10. Drum / Container Trade
11. MOQ Trade
12. Tiered / Volume Pricing
13. Business / Trade Supply

CORE DATA:
- Commercial Mode
- Trade Unit
- MOQ
- Available Quantity
- Wholesale Price
- Bulk Price
- Price Tiers
- Lot Quantity
- Packaging/Trade Pack
- Business Supply Context

RULES:
- Haiduplicate Product Taxonomy.
- Product inabaki kwenye Primary Category yake.
- Wholesale/Bulk si Variant by default.
- Trade Unit si Variant by default.
- MOQ si Variant.
- Price Tier si Variant.
- Bundle si Bulk.
- Unit Engine ni central authority.
- Inventory bado ni canonical Business → Store → Product → SKU → Inventory.
Kwa hiyo sasa:
39 = Condition/Used discovery
40 = Wholesale/Bulk commercial discovery
Na zote mbili zinaweza kukaa juu ya product yoyote bila kuunda duplicate taxonomy.
Inayofuata ni 41 — Import, Export & Cross-Border. Hapo tutafanya correction nyingine muhimu: Imported/Exported si product category; ni Trade/Origin/Market Context, lakini bado 41 inaweza kubaki kama cross-category trade discovery layer.




Ndiyo. Sasa twende CATEGORY 41 — IMPORT, EXPORT & CROSS-BORDER kwa mfumo ule ule tuliokubaliana.
Kwanza, source ya sasa ya Tanzania Trade Portal inaonyesha wazi kwamba Import, Export na Transit ni aina za operation, na portal ina taarifa tofauti za procedures, HS classification, rules of origin, restrictions/prohibitions, permits na documents. Pia ina product-specific procedures kama food, cosmetics, textiles, used motor vehicles, second-hand clothes, spare parts, n.k. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo hapa correction ni kubwa:
CATEGORY 41 — IMPORT, EXPORT & CROSS-BORDER
41. IMPORT, EXPORT & CROSS-BORDER

TYPE:
Cross-Category Trade / Discovery Classification
Si product category ya kawaida.
Bidhaa bado inabaki kwenye Category yake halisi.
________________________________________

CATEGORY 41

Hizi ni trade/discovery classifications, si subcategories za bidhaa.

41.3 EXPORT PRODUCTS

→ Export Products
Trade Portal kwa sasa ina procedures maalum za export kwa bidhaa kama coffee, cashew, tea, cotton, tobacco, flowers, avocados n.k., jambo linaloonyesha kwa nini compliance lazima iwe product + trade-operation dependent, si category moja inayofanana kwa kila bidhaa. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
________________________________________
41.4 CROSS-BORDER
Hii ni broader kuliko Import/Export.
Cross-Border
→ Buyer country ≠ Seller country
Mfano:
        ↓
Kenya Buyer
au:
        ↓
China Seller
Trade direction inaweza kuwa:
IMPORT
EXPORT
lakini Cross-border ni market context.
________________________________________
41.5 TRANSIT
Transit isiwekwe kama Import.
TRANSIT
→ Goods passing through a country
→ Destination is another country
Tanzania Trade Portal yenyewe inaweka Transit kama operation tofauti na Import na Export. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
Kwa hiyo:
Trade Operation
├── Import
├── Export
└── Transit
hii iwe central Trade Operation Dictionary.
________________________________________
41.6 COUNTRY OF ORIGIN
Hii iwe field kuu:
ORIGIN
├── Country
├── Region
├── Manufacturer Country
├── Production Country
└── Origin Evidence
Mfano:
Product
→ Solar Panel

Country of Manufacture
→ China

Seller Location
→ Tanzania
Usichanganye:
Origin ≠ Shipping Location
Origin ≠ Current Stock Location

Mfano:
Seller
→ Dar es Salaam

→ China

→ Dar es Salaam

Destination
→ Arusha
Hii ni bidhaa imported, lakini order yenyewe ni domestic sale.
Hivyo:
≠ Every delivery across Tanzania

└── Allowed Markets

→ Export
→ Destination: Europe

41.12 CERTIFICATE OF ORIGIN
Hii ni Trade Document, si attribute ya kawaida kama color.
CERTIFICATE OF ORIGIN
├── Type
├── Issuing Authority
├── Certificate Number
├── Issue Date
├── Expiry/Validity where applicable
├── Country of Origin
└── Verification Status
Trade Portal ina procedures za Certificate of Origin pamoja na EAC, SADC, AGOA, EU na AfCFTA-related certificates. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
________________________________________
41.13 HS CODE
Hii ni muhimu sana.
HS CLASSIFICATION
├── HS Code
├── HS Version
├── Product Classification
├── Classification Source
└── Verification Status
AI isijiamulie HS code kama fact iliyothibitishwa.
Inaweza kusema:
Suggested HS Code
lakini:
Verified HS Code
iwe confirmed through appropriate trade/customs authority or process.
Trade Portal yenyewe ina search kwa HS Code na ina taarifa kuhusu classification/valuation ya bidhaa kwa customs. (Tanzania Trade Portal)
________________________________________
41.14 CUSTOMS VALUE
Hii isiwe normal product price.
PRODUCT PRICE
≠
CUSTOMS VALUE
Kwa hiyo tusichanganye:
Selling Price
Wholesale Price
Export Price
Import Cost
Customs Value
Landed Cost
Hizi ni financial/trade fields tofauti.
________________________________________
41.15 LANDED COST
Kwa imported product:
Landed Cost
=
Product Cost
+
Freight
+
Insurance where applicable
+
Duties/Taxes/Fees
+
Other applicable costs
Hii ni business calculation, si product attribute.
Na inaweza kubadilika kwa shipment.
________________________________________
41.16 TRADE DOCUMENTS
Tuwe na central Trade Document authority:
TRADE DOCUMENTS
├── Commercial Invoice
├── Packing List
├── Certificate of Origin
├── Customs Declaration
├── Transport Document
├── Permit
├── License
├── Inspection Certificate
├── Certificate of Conformity
├── Phytosanitary Certificate
├── Health Certificate
└── Other Required Documents
Lakini documents zinazohitajika zinategemea product + operation + jurisdiction. Trade Portal inaonyesha mfano wa procedures zinazohusisha customs declaration, certificates, inspection na permits kwa bidhaa tofauti. (Tanzania Trade Portal)

├── Unrestricted
├── Restricted
├── Controlled
├── Prohibited
└── Requires Approval
Lakini status hiyo lazima itoke kwenye authoritative regulatory data/process, si AI guessing.

41.28 FINAL CATEGORY 41
41. IMPORT, EXPORT & CROSS-BORDER

TYPE:
Cross-Category Trade / Discovery Classification

DISCOVERY TYPES:
1. Imported Products
2. Export Products
3. Cross-Border Products
4. Transit Trade
5. Local-to-International
6. International-to-Local
7. Regional Trade
8. Country of Origin
9. Trade Destination
10. Trade Documentation
11. Certificate of Origin
12. Customs / HS Classification
13. Trade Compliance
14. Restricted / Controlled Trade

CORE AUTHORITIES:
- Trade Operation
- Origin
- Destination
- HS Classification
- Trade Documents
- Permits/Licences
- Compliance Status
- Customs/Trade Status
- Cross-Border Logistics

RULES:
- Haiduplicate Product Taxonomy.
- Product inabaki kwenye Primary Category yake.
- Import/Export/Transit ni Trade Operations.
- Origin si Seller Location.
- Destination si Stock Location.
- HS Code si ordinary product attribute.
- Customs Value si Selling Price.
- Trade Compliance ni conditional.
- Trade Documents ni separate authority.
- Logistics inatumia Delivery/Usafir engine iliyopo.
Sasa picha ya 39–41 imekaa hivi:
PRODUCT
   │
   ├── PRIMARY CATEGORY
   │
   ├── CONDITION
   │      └── Category 39 Discovery
   │
   ├── COMMERCIAL MODE
   │      └── Category 40 Discovery
   │
   └── TRADE CONTEXT
          └── Category 41 Discovery
Kwa hiyo bidhaa moja inaweza kuwa:
“Used refurbished laptop, imported from China, sold wholesale in Tanzania”
na mfumo ukaielewa kama:
Category 13 → Computers & IT
Condition → Refurbished
Category 39 → Used/Refurbished Discovery

Commercial Mode → Wholesale
Category 40 → Wholesale Discovery

Trade Operation → Import
Origin → China
Category 41 → Import Discovery

Stock Location → Dar es Salaam
Hakuna duplicate ya laptop hata moja. Hii ndiyo architecture tunayohitaji kabla ya kuingia Category 42. (Tanzania Trade Portal)


Ndiyo. Twende sasa CATEGORY 42 — ARDHI & PROPERTY. Hapa tunahitaji kuwa makini zaidi kuliko categories zilizopita, kwa sababu Property si bidhaa ya kawaida yenye SKU/stock. Ni asset/property listing yenye location, ownership/tenure, matumizi, ukubwa, nyaraka na transaction type.
Kwa hiyo Category 42 iwe Property Authority, lakini bado iingie ndani ya SokoHai bila kuvunja Product/Listing architecture.

Hii inatofautiana na:
variantId
sku

42.34 FINAL CATEGORY 42
42. ARDHI & PROPERTY

TYPE:
Property / Asset Category

MASTER PROPERTY TYPES:
1. Land
2. Plot
3. House
4. Apartment
5. Room
6. Villa
7. Townhouse
8. Commercial Building
9. Office
10. Shop
11. Warehouse
12. Workshop
13. Factory
14. Farm
15. Ranch
16. Hotel
17. Lodge
18. Other Property

CORE DIMENSIONS:
- Property Type
- Land Type / Land Use
- Transaction Type
- Location
- Size / Area
- Dimensions
- Property Features
- Condition
- Availability
- Price
- Rental Terms
- Lease Terms
- Ownership/Tenure Metadata
- Documentation
- Verification
- Development Stage
- Project/Unit Relationship

RULES:
- Property si normal Product/SKU inventory.
- Property ina canonical propertyId.
- Individual property/unit ndiyo asset identity.
- Product zinazotumika kwenye property hazihamii Category 42.
- Farm ≠ crops.
- Ranch ≠ livestock.
- Hotel building ≠ hotel service.
- Property ownership haithibitishwi na seller account pekee.
- AI haiwezi kujitangaza document/ownership/legal verification.
- Exact location inaweza kuwa privacy-controlled.
- Property agent ni role tofauti na delivery agent.
Sasa Categories 39–42 zina architecture hii:
PRODUCT / ASSET
       │
       ├── 39 CONDITION
       │      Used / Refurbished / Open Box...
       │
       ├── 40 COMMERCIAL MODE
       │      Wholesale / Bulk / MOQ...
       │
       ├── 41 TRADE CONTEXT
       │      Import / Export / Cross-Border...
       │
       └── 42 PROPERTY
              Land / House / Apartment /
              Commercial / Farm / Ranch...
Tofauti kubwa: 39–41 ni cross-cutting classifications, wakati 42 ni canonical asset/property domain yenyewe.
Inayofuata kwenye master ni 43 — Madini, Mawe & Resources. Hapo tutahitaji kutenganisha vizuri raw mineral/resource, processed mineral, gemstones, construction stone, mining equipment na mining service bila ku-duplicate Categories 06, 10 na 33.




Sawa 👍
Tukiendelea, CATEGORY 43 — MADINI, MAWE & RESOURCES ndiyo inayofuata.
Tutaiweka kwa mfumo huu wa msingi:
43. MADINI, MAWE & RESOURCES
│
├── Ores & Raw Minerals
├── Precious Metals
├── Gemstones
├── Industrial Minerals
├── Construction Minerals & Stone
├── Aggregates
├── Salt & Mineral Products
├── Mineral Concentrates
├── Processed Mineral Products
├── Mining By-products
└── Mineral Trade
Lakini tutachambua kwa kina ili tusichanganye:
Madini/Resource       → 43
Mbao                  → 06
Cement/Building stone → 10
Mining machine        → 33
Mining service        → Huduma
Jewellery iliyotengenezwa → 07/27/28 kulingana na bidhaa
Pia tutatengeneza Mineral Type, Grade, Purity, Weight, Origin, Processing State, Certification/Documentation, Compliance na Trade Context bila kugeuza kila moja kuwa Variant.
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
Tuko tayari kuijenga 43 kwa undani, kisha 44.







Ndiyo. Nimeangalia pia taarifa za sasa za Wizara ya Madini ili Category 43 isiwe generic tu. Tanzania inatambua makundi kama metallic minerals, gemstones, industrial minerals, energy minerals, strategic/rare-earth minerals na construction minerals/building materials; pia kuna mifumo maalum ya mineral dealing, processing, beneficiation, lapidary na export/import. (Madini)
CATEGORY 43 — MADINI, MAWE & RESOURCES
43. MADINI, MAWE & RESOURCES

TYPE:
Mineral / Natural Resource Product Category
Hapa tofauti na 39–41 ni kwamba 43 ni canonical product category yenyewe. Madini ni bidhaa/resource inayouzwa; lakini ndani yake hatutachanganya machinery, mining service au jewellery iliyokwishatengenezwa.

43. MADINI, MAWE & RESOURCES

Hii inaendana kwa kiasi kikubwa na makundi yanayotumiwa katika nyaraka za Wizara ya Madini, huku construction minerals/building materials zikiwa tofauti na metallic, gemstone, industrial na energy minerals. (Madini)

43.41 COMPLIANCE
Hii ni conditional na high importance.
MINERAL PRODUCT
+
MINERAL TYPE
+
ACTOR
+
TRANSACTION
+
ORIGIN
+
DESTINATION
        ↓
MINERAL COMPLIANCE RESOLVER
Inaweza kuangalia:
Permit
Certificate
Trade Authority
Export/Import Requirements
Restricted Status
Verification
Kwa Tanzania, Ministry material states that authorized miners and holders of dealer licences can export minerals, and mineral import/export is subject to permits; specific licensing categories also exist. (Madini)
Kwa hiyo SokoHai isiache mineral listing kuwa ordinary “weka bidhaa → price → pay” bila conditional compliance flow.

Golden rules
RAW MINERAL
→ 43

PROCESSED MINERAL
→ 43

REFINED METAL
→ 43

GEMSTONE
→ 43

JEWELLERY
→ Jewellery category

MINING MACHINE
→ 33

MINING SERVICE
→ Huduma

MINERAL TRADE
→ 43 + Trade Context

IMPORT/EXPORT
→ Category 41 overlay

WHOLESALE/BULK
→ Category 40 overlay

USED/REFURBISHED EQUIPMENT
→ Category 39 + actual equipment category
Kwa hiyo sasa 43 imekuwa category halisi ya resources, tofauti na 39–41 ambazo ni cross-category overlays. Na nimeweka structure inayolingana na makundi rasmi yanayotumika Tanzania badala ya kutengeneza taxonomy ya kubuni. (Madini)






Sawa. Basi 44 tunaiweka rasmi kuwa OTHER / HAIPO HAPA, lakini kwa maana ya Product Taxonomy Fallback, si General Retail. Hii itakamilisha upande wa BIDHAA.
44. OTHER / HAIPO HAPA
44. OTHER / HAIPO HAPA

TYPE:
Product Taxonomy Fallback / Unclassified Product

PURPOSE:
Kumruhusu seller kuweka bidhaa ambayo haijaweza kupatikana
kwenye canonical Product Taxonomy, bila kumzuia kuuza.
44.1 Muhimu: 44 si “category ya kawaida”
Hatufanyi:
44
└── Other Products
na kuishia hapo.
Badala yake:
USER
 ↓
SEARCH / WRITE / AI
 ↓
01–43 CANONICAL TAXONOMY
 ↓
NO MATCH?
 ↓
44 OTHER
Kwa hiyo 44 ni safety net ya mwisho.
________________________________________
44.2 Mfumo wa Other
44 OTHER
│
├── Other Category
├── Other Subcategory
├── Other Product Type
├── Other Attribute
├── Other Option
└── Other Variant Value
Lakini Product ikifika 44 kwa sababu category yenyewe haijulikani, tunahifadhi maelezo yote ya seller ili baadaye iweze ku-classify.
________________________________________
44.3 Seller experience
Seller anaandika:
“Nauza nini?”
Mfano:
Nauza bidhaa ya asili ya kusafishia meno
System:
Tunatafuta bidhaa yako...
        ↓
AI + Search
        ↓
Hakuna canonical match ya uhakika
        ↓
44 OTHER
System imwambie:
Hatujapata sehemu sahihi bado. Unaweza kuendelea kuiweka kama Other.
Kisha:
[________________]

Unaweza kueleza zaidi:
[________________]

Picha:
[ + Ongeza picha ]

Bei:
[________________]

[Endelea]
________________________________________
44.4 Lakini mfumo usikate tamaa haraka
Order ya classification iwe:
1. Exact Product Match
        ↓
2. Search / Synonym Match
        ↓
3. Category Match
        ↓
4. Subcategory Match
        ↓
5. AI Classification
        ↓
6. Attribute-based Classification
        ↓
7. Seller Confirmation
        ↓
8. OTHER
Hivyo Other inatumika mwisho kabisa.
________________________________________
44.5 Other Category
Ikiwa kabisa hatujui category:
Category
→ 44 OTHER / HAIPO HAPA
Lakini lazima kuwe na:
customCategoryName
productDescription
sellerInput
suggestedCategory
classificationConfidence
classificationStatus
________________________________________
44.6 Other Subcategory
Ikiwa category inajulikana:
Category
→ 01 Chakula

Subcategory
→ Other Food Product
Hivyo si lazima tupeleke product yote Category 44.
Hii ni rule muhimu sana:
Other itumike kwenye level ya chini kabisa ambayo haijulikani.

kwa sababu tunajua product iko kwenye Urembo.

44.10 Other Variant Value

"5XL"

→ Other / Custom
Value
→ 5XL
Lakini haimaanishi automatic kuwa 5XL imekuwa canonical variant rule.
________________________________________
44.11 Pending Taxonomy
Kila Other iweze kuwa signal:
OTHER RECORD
│
├── Seller Input
├── Product
├── Current Classification
├── Suggested Classification
├── Evidence
├── Frequency
├── Status
└── Review History
Status:
PENDING
UNDER_REVIEW
MATCHED
MERGED
PROMOTED
REJECTED
________________________________________
44.12 Mfano wa promotion
Wauzaji 1:
Mchele wa aina X
AI haijui.
→ Other Grain
Wauzaji 2:
Aina X rice
Wauzaji 3:
X rice
System inaona repeated pattern.
Other
 ↓
Repeated Signals
 ↓
Taxonomy Review
 ↓
Canonical Product Type

→ X Rice

Other
kama Other ni category inayoshindana na categories halisi.

Other products
→ fallback/unclassified listings
inaweza kuwa internal filter/admin classification, na si lazima iwe prominent kwa buyer.

X product

        ↓

        ↓

        ↓
Relevant results
Lakini search ranking isiwape Other priority kwa sababu tu ni Other.

Kanuni kuu ya 44
CANONICAL FIRST
      ↓
ADAPTIVE CLASSIFICATION
      ↓
LOWEST POSSIBLE OTHER
      ↓
CUSTOM VALUE
      ↓
PENDING TAXONOMY SIGNAL
      ↓
REVIEW
      ↓
MERGE / PROMOTE
Kwa hiyo seller anaweza kusema tu:
“Nauza nyama.”
→ mfumo unampeleka 01 → Nyama.
“Nauza maziwa.”
→ 01 → Maziwa & Dairy.
“Nauza kitu fulani ambacho sioni.”
→ mfumo unajaribu kukitambua → ikishindikana, 44 Other → seller anaendelea kuuza bila kuzuiwa.
Na baadaye Other hiyo inaweza kuinuliwa kuwa canonical taxonomy bila kuvunja products zilizokwishawekwa.
Hivyo 44 sasa imefungwa rasmi kama OTHER / HAIPO HAPA — Product Taxonomy Fallback.
